*Jiahao Lin,Alish Kanani,Sangwan Lee,Jaehyun Park,Umit Ogras*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: HYDRA是一个面向混合Transformer-Mamba大语言模型在异构Chiplet系统上服务的综合设计空间探索框架，联合优化芯片组成、布局、带宽配置、动态批处理和运行时调度，相比SOTA基线平均提升1.55倍吞吐量并降低43.7%的首令牌时延。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合Transformer-Mamba架构的大语言模型具有异构计算和通信模式，传统硬件加速难以高效支持。Chiplet架构虽提供可扩展方案，但其静态架构配置和动态运行时策略构成的设计空间过于庞大，无法穷举探索，迫切需要联合设计空间探索方法。

Method: HYDRA框架联合探索芯片组成（chiplet composition）、布局（placement）、片间带宽配置（inter-chiplet bandwidth provisioning）、动态批处理（dynamic batching）和运行时调度（runtime scheduling）。核心组件包括：通信感知布局、动态批处理、弹性任务调度，以及一个基于马尔可夫模型的快速性能估计器，用于捕获多租户运行时动态以实现高效准确的探索。

Result: 在所有工作负载下，HYDRA相比SOTA基线平均提升1.55倍吞吐量，并将time-to-first-token（TTFT）降低43.7%；吞吐量提升最高可达2.3倍。

Conclusion: 结果表明，架构与运行时策略的协同设计（co-design）对于在异构Chiplet系统上实现高效的大规模LLM服务至关重要。

Abstract: Hybrid Transformer-Mamba large language models (LLMs) enhance long-context efficiency, but their heterogeneous computation and communication patterns complicate efficient hardware acceleration. Chiplet-based architectures offer a scalable solution by integrating specialized compute and memory units. However, the design space spanning static architectural configurations and dynamic runtime policies is prohibitively large to explore exhaustively. To address this challenge, we present HYDRA, a comprehensive design space exploration framework for hybrid LLM serving on heterogeneous chiplet systems. HYDRA jointly explores chiplet composition, placement, inter-chiplet bandwidth provisioning, dynamic batching, and runtime scheduling. It integrates communication-aware placement, dynamic batching, elastic task scheduling, and a fast Markov-based performance estimator that captures multi-tenant runtime dynamics for efficient and accurate exploration. Across all workloads, HYDRA delivers 1.55x the throughput and 43.7 percent lower time-to-first-token on average, with throughput gains reaching up to 2.3x compared to state-of-the-art baselines. These results highlight that co-designing architecture and runtime policies is critical for efficient large-scale LLM serving on heterogeneous chiplet systems.

</details>


### [269] [Exact Multistate Reliability and Upgrade Design for Heterogeneous HBM Systems via Threshold-Pruned BAT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19471)
*Wei-Chang Yeh*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了HBM系统中基于多状态可靠性的精确框架，每个服务单元携带有限带宽状态集，可靠性定义为聚合带宽满足需求的概率。提出阈值剪枝多状态二叉树算法（TP-mBAT），在可通约基准上273次DP更新对比362,506次节点访问；在不可通约基准上两者状态空间相当（6,862节点vs 6,861更新），但保留状态从412,121降至17，峰值存储从25.02 MB降至1,152 B。还提出精确概率传递灵敏度分析与保留单元下限模型，忽视依赖关系会高估可靠性8.73个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HBM系统不仅存在全服务或完全隔离，还可能表现出部分服务，即控制器可见的服务单元可能因子信道隔离、通道重映射或保护开销而提供全带宽、降级带宽或零带宽。现有二值k-out-of-n模型不足以刻画这种多状态部分服务现象，需要建立精确的多状态可靠性分析框架。

Method: (1) 提出多状态可靠性框架，每个服务单元携带任意有限带宽状态集，可靠性为聚合带宽满足需求的概率；(2) 恢复二值k-out-of-n模型为特例，推导封闭形式二值映射误差关系以量化平均带宽失真并提供筛选检验；(3) 提出阈值剪枝多状态二叉加法树（TP-mBAT）算法用于精确单阈值评估，采用固定网格动态规划作为可通约场景的替代方案；(4) 提出精确概率传递灵敏度分析及保留单元下限模型的第三类剪枝规则；(5) 建立潜在包状态混合模型捕捉共享应力依赖。

Result: 在16单元可通约基准中，TP-mBAT需362,506次节点访问而剪枝DP仅需273次更新。在14单元不可通约基准中，TP-mBAT与携带相同阈值规则的DP展开相同状态空间（6,862节点vs 6,861更新），但保留条目仅17个对比412,121个概率状态，峰值存储从25.02 MB降至1,152 B。忽略依赖关系的模型高估可靠性8.73个百分点。

Conclusion: 该多状态可靠性框架及TP-mBAT算法能在不同场景下提供精确且内存高效的评价手段：可通约紧凑网格用剪枝DP更优，不可通约场景TP-mBAT大幅减少内存占用而不削弱对照基准；概率传递灵敏度分析和保留单元下限模型进一步丰富了精确剪枝与依赖建模能力。

Abstract: High Bandwidth Memory (HBM) systems can exhibit partial service rather than only full service or complete isolation: a controller-visible service unit may deliver full, reduced, or zero bandwidth because of sub-channel isolation, lane remapping, or protection overhead. This paper develops an exact multistate reliability framework in which each service unit carries an arbitrary finite set of bandwidth states and reliability is the probability that aggregate delivered bandwidth meets a demand. The binary k-out-of-n model is recovered as a special case, while closed-form binary-mapping error relations quantify mean-bandwidth distortion and provide a screening test for the simpler abstraction. For exact single-threshold evaluation a threshold-pruned multistate Binary-Addition-Tree (TP-mBAT) algorithm is proposed. It is deliberately regime-specific: fixed-grid dynamic programming is preferable on a compact common grid, where a 16-unit commensurate control required 273 pruned-DP updates versus 362,506 TP-mBAT node visits. On a reproducible 14-unit incommensurate benchmark, TP-mBAT is compared against a dynamic program carrying the same threshold rules, so that no baseline is weakened. Both expand the same state space, 6,862 nodes against 6,861 updates, and the separation lies entirely in retained state: 17 traversal entries against 412,121 probability states at central demand, reducing measured peak storage from 25.02 MB to 1,152 B. An exact probability-transfer sensitivity identifies when moving mass from a degraded state to a higher-bandwidth state changes system success, and a reserved-unit floor model admits a third exact pruning rule that is vacuous without such floors. A latent package-state mixture captures shared stress, where ignoring dependence overstates reliability by 8.73 percentage points.

</details>


### [270] [A Thread-Register Decoupled GPU Execution Model for Efficient Tensor Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19628)
*Zihan Liu,Jingwen Leng,Yangjie Zhou,Yitong Ding,Guanlin Zhu,Yilu Huang,Chiheng Jin,Chen Zhang,Shixuan Sun,Yu Feng,Anbang Wu,Minyi Guo,Jian Weng,Jiajin Tu,Junsong Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: FIBER是一种新型GPU架构，通过引入与私有寄存器所有权解耦的"fiber"执行实例，实现动态并行扩展和细粒度寄存器级数据流调度，有效解决了现代AI工作负载中GEMM与非GEMM操作交错带来的固定并行度和粗粒度调度瓶颈，在混合精度LLM推理场景下实现最高2.49x的kernel级加速和2.25x的端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代GPU中的Tensor Core吞吐持续增长（从Ampere的寄存器供给演进到Hopper/Blackwell的免冗余内存供给），但完整张量计算流水线的编排仍面临挑战。根本瓶颈在于固定并行度和粗粒度调度——这两种限制被现代AI工作负载中多样化的非GEMM操作与GEMM交错执行所暴露。

Method: 提出FIBER架构，扩展GPU的SIMT执行模型。核心执行实例fiber与私有寄存器所有权解耦，仅携带最小控制状态，通过共享视图访问SM寄存器。具体实现包括：扩展ISA和微架构以支持共享寄存器寻址、无冲突操作数分发，以及基于fiber的程序映射；编译器层面实现fiber程序映射支持。这实现了动态并行度扩展、细粒度寄存器级数据流调度，并为矩阵操作数供给提供了免冗余替代方案。

Result: 在典型混合精度LLM服务场景下，FIBER在Ampere上实现2.25x端到端加速（原始FP16计算为1.15x），在Hopper和Blackwell上分别实现1.8x和2.09x加速，kernel级加速最高达2.49x。

Conclusion: 通过将执行实例从私有寄存器所有权解耦，FIBER实现了GPU计算资源的动态和细粒度编排，有效弥补了Tensor Core硬件吞吐与实际AI工作负载利用率之间的鸿沟，为现代AI加速器设计提供了新的架构方向。

Abstract: Modern GPUs increasingly integrate Tensor Cores into the execution pipeline. Although aggregate tensor throughput continues to grow, aided by an operand supply that has evolved from register-based in Ampere to redundancy-free, memory-based in Hopper and Blackwell, efficiently orchestrating the complete tensor compute pipeline for the modern AI workloads remains challenging. We identify the fundamental bottlenecks as fixed parallelism and coarse-grained scheduling, both of which are exposed by modern AI workloads that interleave diverse non-GEMM operations with GEMM. To orchestrate tensor computation efficiently, we propose FIBER, a new architecture that extends the GPU SIMT (single instruction, multiple thread) model. Its basic execution instance, the \emph{fiber}, is decoupled from private register ownership, carrying only minimal control state while accessing an SM's registers through a shared view. This enables dynamic parallelism scaling, fine-grained register-level dataflow scheduling, and offers a redundancy-free alternative for matrix operand supply. We extend the ISA, microarchitecture, and compiler to realize shared-register addressing, conflict-free operand delivery, and fiber-based program mapping. Under a typical mixed-precision LLM serving scenario, FIBER achieves a 2.25x end-to-end speedup on Ampere (1.15x for the original FP16 computation), with 1.8x and 2.09x on Hopper and Blackwell respectively, and kernel-level gains up to 2.49x.

</details>


### [271] [Automated Estimation of MBIST Area and Test Time in Heterogeneous Memory IPs via Stacked Ensemble Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19705)
*Chee Jin Teoh,Ab Al-Hadi Ab Rahman,Johnny Kee Hui Wong,Premkumar A/L Kesavan Prabagaran,Muhammad Nadzir Marsono,Nuzhat Khan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于监督学习的框架，直接从RTL级设计参数预测MBIST面积开销和测试时间，无需传统综合与测试模式生成，面积预测准确率达90.68%，测试时间预测准确率达96.80%（±10%误差范围内），分别比基线方法提升8.53%和48.80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式存储器在现代SoC设计中占据大量面积，MBIST虽能保障存储器可靠性但引入额外面积和测试时间开销。传统开销估算依赖完整RTL综合和测试模式生成，耗时且资源密集，无法满足设计规划阶段的快速估算需求。

Method: 构建两个独立的有监督学习流程：(1) 面积预测——对输入特征（存储器数量、字宽、地址深度、端口配置、时钟域）依次进行多项式扩展、对数变换和缩放，然后使用XGBoost、LightGBM和神经网络构成堆叠集成模型，以梯度提升作为元学习器；(2) 测试时间预测——使用Ridge回归组合XGBoost和LightGBM，并通过100次Optuna试验进行超参数调优。数据集通过Synopsys Design Compiler和Intel增强版MBIST工具MINT生成，面积4,470个样本、测试时间624个样本。

Result: 面积预测在±10%误差范围内达到90.68%准确率，测试时间预测达到96.80%准确率，相较基线方法分别提升8.53%和48.80%。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在无需综合的前提下快速估算MBIST成本，显著加速设计规划阶段的决策过程，有效支持存储器IP开发中的设计权衡分析。

Abstract: Embedded memories occupy a large portion of modern System-on-Chip (SoC) designs, especially in high-performance applications such as artificial intelligence and edge computing. Memory Built-In Self-Test (MBIST) is commonly used to ensure memory reliability, but it introduces additional area and test time overhead. Accurate early estimation of these overheads is important during design planning, yet conventional methods rely on full Register Transfer Level (RTL) synthesis and test pattern generation, which are slow and resource-intensive. This study proposes a supervised learning framework that predicts MBIST area and test time directly from RTL-level design parameters without synthesis. A dataset of 4,470 samples for area and 624 for test time was generated using Synopsys Design Compiler and MINT, an Intel-enhanced MBIST tool. Input features include memory count, word width, address depth, port configuration, and clock domains. For area prediction, the features are processed through polynomial expansion, log transformation, and scaling, followed by a stacked ensemble model using XGBoost, LightGBM, and a Neural Network with Gradient Boosting as the meta-learner. For test time, XGBoost and LightGBM are combined using Ridge Regression, with hyperparameters tuned through a 100-trial Optuna search. The models achieved 90.68% accuracy for area and 96.80% for test time within a +/-10% margin, improving over baseline methods by 8.53% and 48.80% respectively. The results show that this approach enables faster estimation of MBIST costs and supports more efficient design decisions in memory IP development.

</details>


### [272] [Energy-Efficient Visual Inspection with FFT-Based CNNs and Adaptive Floating-Point Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19837)
*Lukas Krupp,Marco Groß,Michael Graichen,Kim Ulrich,Norbert Wehn*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文研究在工业CPU-FPGA平台上使用降低精度的浮点运算进行基于FFT的CNN推理，结合后训练FP8量化，并通过层间指数偏置选择将准确率从80.33%提升至84.13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在嵌入式/工业部署场景中，CNN推理需要同时兼顾高吞吐量和高能效。FFT-based卷积可以降低计算复杂度，但浮点精度降低会导致准确率损失，因此需要探索在不牺牲太多精度的情况下实现高效FPGA加速的方案。

Method: 提出两种FPGA导向优化方法：(1) 在FFT内部进行渐进式偏置调整（PBA）；(2) 在CNN各层之间进行指数偏置选择。将FFT-based卷积与自适应后训练FP8量化相结合，并在采用串行radix-2^2 SDF FFT模块的LeNet-5加速器上实现，使用工业故障检测数据集进行评估。

Result: 权重缩放优于PBA方法；层间偏置优化在不改变数据通路宽度的情况下将准确率从80.33%提升至84.13%；相比CPU-only推理，FPGA实现的能效提升约2.5倍。

Conclusion: 层间指数偏置选择是一种有效且低成本（不修改硬件数据通路）的FP8低精度量化补偿策略；整体方案在保持可接受准确率的同时实现了显著的能效优势，适合工业场景中的CNN部署。

Abstract: This paper investigates reduced-precision floating-point arithmetic for FFT-based CNN inference on an industrial CPU-FPGA platform. We combine FFT-based convolution with adaptive post-training FP8 quantization and evaluate two FPGA-oriented optimization methods: progressive bias adjustment (PBA) within the FFT and layer-wise exponent-bias selection across the CNN. The methods are implemented in a LeNet-5 accelerator using serial radix-$2^2$ SDF FFT modules and evaluated on an industrial fault detection dataset. Results show that weight scaling outperforms PBA, while layer-wise bias optimization increases the accuracy from 80.33% to 84.13% without modifying the datapath width. Compared with CPU-only inference, the FPGA achieves approximately 2.5$\times$ higher energy efficiency.

</details>


### [273] [Experimental Verification of Fast Voltage Droop Correction Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19954)
*Shreyas Srinivas,Ian W. Jones,Carsten Schulze,Milos Krstic,Christoph Lenzen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种基于IHP 130nm工艺的全数字快速自适应电压跌落响应电路，并在测试芯片上进行了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为节能而最小化保护带（guard bands）的趋势使得电压跌落（voltage droops）成为当今VLSI电路工作频率的关键限制因素。动态调整时钟频率时，检测和存储电压跌落存在的设备会面临亚稳态（metastability）挑战。

Method: 实现了一个快速全数字（all-digital）电路，用于对电压跌落的自适应响应，采用IHP 130nm工艺制造。

Result: 在测试芯片上实验验证了设计的功能性。

Conclusion: 所提出的全数字自适应电路能够有效应对电压跌落问题，并在实际芯片上得到验证。

Abstract: Due to the trend towards minimizing guard bands for energy saving purposes, voltage droops are a key limiting factor for the operational frequency of today's VLSI circuits. Adapting clock frequencies dynamically presents the challenge of metastability in the device that detects and stores the existence of voltage droops. We present an implementation of a fast all-digital circuit for adaptive response to droops using the IHP 130 nm process. The description of the design is presented in an accompanying paper. We experimentally validate the functionality of the design on a test chip.

</details>


### [274] [ODEONN: A Digital ODE Solver Architecture for Oscillatory Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20110)
*Bram F. Haverkort,Aida Todri-Sanial*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出ODEONN，一种模块化、可扩展的通用数字振荡神经网络架构，支持复数耦合，并引入一种仅需标准方法一半硬件资源的正弦函数近似方法。相比全精度软件仿真，性能退化小于2%，且在能耗延迟积上实现了45倍的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 振荡神经网络（ONNs）是AI和组合优化问题的替代计算范式，但现有数字架构通常针对特定应用设计，缺乏通用性。此外，支持复数耦合的完全数字ONN架构此前尚未出现。

Method: 提出一种模块化、可扩展的通用数字ONN架构（ODEONN），首次支持复数耦合；同时引入一种新的正弦函数近似方法，相比标准方法节省一半硬件资源。

Result: 与全精度软件仿真相比，性能退化小于2%；相比在传统硬件上运行的软件仿真，能耗延迟积降低了45倍。

Conclusion: 定点量化和近似波形对计算精度的影响很小，ODEONN在保持高精度的同时实现了显著的能效提升，可广泛适用于多种ONN应用。

Abstract: Oscillatory Neural Networks (ONNs) are an alternative computing paradigm for AI and combinatorial optimization problems. However, digital architectures are often designed for specific applications of ONNs. This work introduces a modular and scalable architecture called ODEONN that is generic to multiple applications of ONNs, and to the best of our knowledge, is the first fully digital ONN to also support complex-valued coupling. Additionally, an approximation of the sine function is introduced that uses half of the hardware resources compared to standard methods. The performance of ODEONN is compared with a full-precision software simulation, where a performance degradation of less than $2\%$ is shown. Therefore, we conclude that the fixed-point quantization and the approximated waveform affect the accuracy of computation by only a small amount. Furthermore, ODEONN shows a 45$\times$ reduction in energy-delay product over the software simulation running on conventional hardware.

</details>


### [275] [A Resource-Efficient CNN-Based EEG Auditory Attention Decoding ASIC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20198)
*Qier Ma,Richard George,Stefan Scholze,Jehn Constantin,Tobias Reichenbach,Christian Mayr*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一款面向真实场景的EEG听觉注意力解码ASIC芯片，集成量化CNN推理引擎和Pearson相关分类器，在GF22FDX 22nm CMOS工艺上实现，总面积2.09mm²，功耗仅0.4941mW，推理延迟7.34ms，为助听设备中的鸡尾酒会问题提供高效硬件平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嘈杂环境中追踪目标说话人（鸡尾酒会问题）对人工耳蜗用户尤其困难，近年虽有基于EEG和神经网络的听觉注意力解码研究，但缺乏资源高效、可实时部署的硬件实现，因此需要设计专用的低功耗ASIC芯片。

Method: 采用量化CNN推理引擎与Pearson相关分类器相结合的系统架构；通过流式执行（streaming execution）、片上缓冲（on-chip buffering）和存储器高效数据流（memory-efficient dataflow）降低硬件成本，同时保证实时性能。

Result: 在GF22FDX 22nm CMOS工艺上完成全面实现，总面积2.09mm²（1264μm × 1654μm），其中CNN推理引擎和流式分类引擎仅占0.076mm²；在0.55V核心电压下功耗为0.4941mW，推理延迟为7.34ms。

Conclusion: 该ASIC为EEG听觉注意力解码提供了高能效的硬件平台，特别适用于助听类应用场景，展示了在极低功耗和面积约束下实现实时解码的可行性。

Abstract: Following a target speaker in a noisy environment, commonly known as the cocktail party problem, remains particularly challenging for cochlear implant (CI) users. Recent studies have explored EEG-based auditory attention decoding (AAD) using neural networks to enhance hearing assistance. This paper presents a resource-efficient ASIC for real-time EEG-based auditory attention decoding by integrating a quantized CNN inference engine and a Pearson-correlation classifier. The proposed architecture employs streaming execution, on-chip buffering, and memory-efficient dataflow to reduce hardware cost while maintaining real-time performance.
  The proposed ASIC has been fully implemented in GF22FDX 22-nm CMOS technology, occupying a total silicon area of 2.09 mm$^2$(1264$μ$m x 1654$μ$m), with the CNN inference engine and streaming classification engine requiring only 0.076 mm$^2$. Operating at a core voltage of 0.55 V, the design achieves a power consumption of 0.4941 mW and an inference latency of 7.34 ms, providing an energy-efficient hardware platform for EEG-based auditory attention decoding in hearing-assistance applications.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.GN'></div>

# q-bio.GN [[Back]](#toc)

### [276] [Hepatitis C Virus Genotyping with a Transformer Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19415)
*Ariella Aro,Taimá Furuyama,Marcelo R. S. Briones,Luis Mário R. Janini,Isabel M. V. Guedes de Carvalho,Fernando Antoneli*

Main category: q-bio.GN

TL;DR: 本研究评估了基于Transformer的模型在微调后对丙型肝炎病毒（HCV）基因型和亚型分类的性能。使用2,881条来自Los Alamos HCV序列数据库的全基因组序列（涵盖基因型1至6），发现采用均匀片段化和平衡样本分布的微调策略在基因型分类中精确率可达98.48%-100%，亚型分类准确率和精确率分别达99.89%和99.87%；而不平衡的片段化策略会导致性能大幅下降（精确率仅48.12%）。研究表明，当微调数据集经过适当处理时，Transformer模型在HCV基因型分类中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Transformer模型在遗传序列分类任务中的适用性，具体评估其预测HCV基因型和亚型的能力，以验证其作为基因分类工具的潜力。

Method: 使用2,881条HCV全基因组序列（基因型1至6及其所有确认亚型，排除样本量不足的基因型7和8），在多个数据集上进行微调实验。不同数据集在片段化方法、每文件数据量和标注方式上有所区别，通过比较不同配置下的分类性能来确定最佳微调策略。

Result: 基因型分类中，均匀片段化和平衡样本分布的微调策略精确率达98.48%-100%；而导致数据不平衡的片段化策略精确率仅48.12%，尤其在基因型5预测中错误率较高。亚型分类最佳精确率99.89%，准确率99.87%。包含额外基因型的模型因任务复杂度增加而性能略有下降。

Conclusion: 当微调数据集包含适当片段化、合理分布和正确标注的基因序列时，Transformer神经网络能够实现高性能，是HCV基因型和亚型分类的一种有前景的方法。

Abstract: This study aims to explore the applicability of Transformer-based models for genetic sequence classification by evaluating their performance in predicting hepatitis C virus (HCV) genotypes and subtypes after fine-tuning. A total of 2,881 HCV whole-genome sequences obtained from the Los Alamos HCV Sequence Database were used, including genotypes 1 to 6 and all confirmed subtypes. Genotypes 7 and 8 were excluded due to an insufficient number of samples. The fine-tuning process was based on several datasets that differed in fragmentation method, data volume per file, and labeling. In genotype classification, fine-tuning strategies employing homogeneous fragmentation and balanced sample distribution resulted in higher performance, with precision ranging from 98.48% to 100%. In contrast, fine-tuning conducted using a fragmentation strategy that caused data imbalance, along with an arbitrary distribution of samples across training files, achieved a precision of 48.12%, which is considered low compared with other models. This configuration, which was also manually evaluated, resulted in a high error rate in genotype 5 prediction due to its low frequency in the datasets used. In subtype classification, the best-performing fine-tuning approach achieved 99.89% accuracy and 99.87% precision. Models that included additional genotypes showed a slight decrease in performance due to the increased complexity of the task. This study demonstrates that, when fine-tuning datasets contain properly fragmented, distributed, and labeled genetic sequences, Transformer-based neural networks can achieve high performance and are a promising approach for HCV genotype and subtype classification.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [277] [The Connectome and the Quest for the Functional Logic of the Drosophila Early Olfactory System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19290)
*Aurel A. Lazar,Yiyin Zhou*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文回顾了果蝇早期嗅觉系统（EOS）过去十五年的连接组与突触组数据，指出前馈通路嵌入在由大规模多输入多输出神经元构成的密集局部反馈回路中。作者认为理解这些反馈回路的抽象机制是揭示嗅觉电路功能逻辑的基础，并主张定量理解功能逻辑需要显式建模自然环境中气味对象，同时考虑实时因果级联的处理框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管果蝇早期嗅觉系统的密集电子显微镜连接组已提供了每个细胞类型、神经元和突触的高质量可视化，但接线图本身并不能揭示神经电路的功能逻辑。需要超越静态连接图，理解嵌入在局部反馈回路中的功能原理。

Method: 系统综述过去十五年间EOS连接组与突触组数据集，识别前馈通路之外的密集局部反馈结构；提出以反馈回路抽象（multi-input multi-output neurons）为核心的分析框架；引入气味对象模型定义嗅觉信息处理的语义与句法；强调因果性驱动的实时级联处理视角。

Result: 揭示前馈通路嵌入于大规模局部反馈回路之中；提出反馈回路抽象是理解输入/输出变换功能逻辑的基础；论证需要自然环境中气味对象的显式模型与新距离度量来支持联想记忆操作；指出因果性约束下的实时级联处理框架是必要的。

Conclusion: 果蝇早期嗅觉系统的功能逻辑不能仅从静态连接图读出，而需要结合反馈回路抽象、气味语义模型和因果实时级联框架进行系统定量理解。

Abstract: In recent decades, the early olfactory system (EOS) of the fruit fly has become a leading model for studying olfactory processing and associative memory, owing in part to a well-characterized feedforward pathway that feeds the processes underlying associative memory and by examining the role played by a handful of neurons and synapses. The recent completion of dense electron-microscopy connectomes provides high quality visualizations of every cell type, neuron, and synapse along the early olfactory pathway. Yet a wiring diagram, however complete, does not by itself reveal the functional logic of a neural circuit. Reviewing the EOS connectome and synaptome datasets of the past fifteen years, we note that the feedforward pathway is embedded in dense local feedback circuits of large scale multi-input multi-output neurons. A systematic understanding of feedback loop abstractions, and their capacity to govern the input/output transformations at each neuropil stage, is the underlying foundation of the functional logic of the early olfactory circuits. In addition, we argue that a quantitative account of the functional logic requires an explicit model of the odorants present in the natural environment. Consisting of odorant objects, such a model defines the semantics and syntax of olfactory information processing, and calls for new distance measures for classifying the odorant semantics in support of associative memory operations. Furthermore, odor information processing must abide by causality, treating the circuit as a real-time, stage-by-stage cascade of giant local feedback loops.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [278] [APPROVE: Visual End-User-in-the-Loop Robot Programming with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19281)
*Bijan Kavousian,Miray Özakkas,Josefine Monnet,Oliver Petrovic,Christian Brecher*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出APPROVE，一个基于LLM的多模态端用户编程框架，集成自然语言输入与块状编程界面，通过显式用户确认步骤增强透明度、意图对齐和可复用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人编程需要专家知识，且现有LLM自动生成机器人程序的方法存在缺乏透明度、无法确保与用户意图对齐、以及缺乏复用支持等局限性。

Method: APPROVE框架将自然语言输入与Blockly块状界面结合，生成程序以块状形式可视化展示，用户可在执行前确认、修改或拒绝；已确认的功能存入库中以供复用，逐步构建可靠的程序组件库。

Result: 该方法提供了一种以人为中心的LLM机器人编程设计，强调用户信任、意图对齐和可复用性。

Conclusion: 通过引入显式用户确认步骤和程序库复用机制，APPROVE有效解决了LLM机器人编程中透明度不足和意图对齐缺失的问题，为面向非专家的机器人编程提供了更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Programming robots remains challenging for non-experts, as traditional methods require expert knowledge and even block-based interfaces often lack flexibility. Recent work has explored Large Language Models (LLMs) to automatically generate robot programs from natural language, but these systems remain limited by a lack of transparency, missing mechanisms to ensure alignment with user intent, and little support for reuse. We present APPROVE (AI-Powered Programming for Robots with Visual End-User Feedback), an LLM-based multi-modal end-user programming framework that integrates natural language input with a block-based interface and an explicit user confirmation step. Generated programs are visualized using a block-based interface in Blockly, allowing users to confirm, modify, or reject them before execution. Confirmed functions are stored in a library for reuse, gradually building a set of reliable program components. Our approach contributes a human-centered design for LLM-based robot programming that emphasizes user trust, intent alignment, and reusability.

</details>


### [279] [The Missing Touch: Spatially Distributed Tactile Feedback Brings Teleoperation Closer to Human Dexterity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19372)
*Rohan Kota,Gregory Reardon,J. Edward Colgate*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过将32自由度触觉指尖显示器与2自由度双边力反馈遥操作器配对，证明空间分布触觉反馈能显著提升遥操作性能——不仅加速任务完成，还将遥操作轨迹与自然人类行为的偏差缩小29-79%，并压缩遥操作运动的状态空间分布，有助于训练自主机器人策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作在机器人演示数据采集中日益广泛使用，但遥操作性能远低于人类手的灵巧度，即使对基本任务也是如此。作者认为缺乏空间分布触觉反馈是造成这一性能差距的关键因素。

Method: 使用一个2自由度双边力反馈遥操作操纵器，配合32自由度触觉指尖显示器。实验条件为在遥控机械臂上的局部变形被忠实地复现到操作者指尖时，对比有无分布式触觉反馈的效果；同时通过改变位移量化的精细程度来调节触觉反馈分辨率，观察对运动状态空间分布的影响。

Result: 当远端机械臂的分布式接触信息被复现时，操作性能显著提升：任务完成速度加快，校正动作和任务完成步骤减少，遥操作轨迹与自然人类轨迹的偏差缩小29-79%；提高触觉反馈分辨率可压缩遥操作运动的状态空间分布，这与自主策略训练效果改善相关。

Conclusion: 空间分布触觉反馈对于弥合人类与遥操作灵巧度之间的差距至关重要，也是训练下一代自主机器人所必需的。

Abstract: A fundamental challenge in robotic teleoperation is enabling an operator to control a remote robot as effortlessly and intuitively as their own hands. Despite the growing use of teleoperation to collect demonstration data for training autonomous robot policies, teleoperated robot performance still falls significantly short of human dexterity, even for basic tasks. Here, we present evidence that a key factor contributing to this performance gap is the absence of spatially distributed tactile feedback. Using a two-degree-of-freedom (DoF) bilateral force-feedback telemanipulator paired with a 32-DoF tactile fingertip display, we show that operator performance improves significantly when localized deformations on the remote manipulator are faithfully reproduced on the operator's fingertip. In a series of teleoperation tasks, reproducing distributed contact information not only accelerated task performance but also brought teleoperated movements closer to natural human behavior by minimizing corrective actions and task completion steps, thereby reducing the deviation between teleoperated and natural trajectories by 29$\unicode{x2013}$79%. Furthermore, we found that increasing the resolution of the tactile feedback$\unicode{x2014}$by refining how finely the measured displacements were quantized for reproduction$\unicode{x2014}$compressed the state-space distribution of teleoperated motions, which has been associated with improved training outcomes for autonomous robot policies. Together, these results suggest that spatially distributed tactile feedback is essential for closing the gap between human and teleoperated dexterity and training the next generation of autonomous robots.

</details>


### [280] [Learning the Right Abstraction: Neural Reduced Dynamics for Complex Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19375)
*Harry Zhang,Dan Negrut*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出神经约化动力学（NRD）框架：用数据驱动的低保真快速仿真替代计算昂贵的高保真物理仿真，在冻结的约化动力学模型中训练RL策略，再回到高保真仿真器验证。在HMMWV轨迹跟踪和履带车+机械臂目标到达等任务上，所有策略均成功迁移回高保真仿真器，且速度比高保真仿真快约4个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真具身AI仿真器对复杂机器人系统评估真实，但计算成本过高，无法直接用于大规模强化学习。作者主张使用精度稍低但速度更快的仿真（如基于神经网络动力学的模型），并指出其实际价值在于学习"正确的抽象"——保留控制相关物理的约化状态，同时支持高吞吐量的策略学习。

Method: NRD框架的三个要点：1）将模型传播的状态与可作为输入提供或可解析恢复的量分离；2）在冻结（frozen）的已学习动力学模型内完全训练策略；3）回传高保真仿真器验证。通过两个案例研究实现三控制任务，包括基于地形的HMMWV轨迹跟踪（刚性、崎岖、可变形CRM地形）和履带车+前置铰接臂的目标到达任务。

Result: 所有策略均成功迁移回高保真仿真器。单个策略在无地形输入条件下，在所有三种地形上的中位数和均值跟踪误差均低于单地形专家策略（包括零样本崎岖地形）。履带车100/100目标到达，机械臂97/100目标到达，且零接触或关节限位违规。NRD模型比高保真仿真场景速度快约4个数量级，使迭代在线策略学习变得可行。

Conclusion: 神经约化动力学可以作为连接"精确但昂贵"物理仿真与"可扩展"机器人学习之间的桥梁。学习正确状态抽象是使低保真神经网络动力学模型在复杂机器人控制中具有实际价值的关键，其速度优势使大规模在线策略学习变得实际可行，同时策略仍能在高保真环境中可靠地工作。

Abstract: High-fidelity embodied AI simulators provide realistic evaluation of complex robotic systems, but their computational cost limits their direct use for large-scale reinforcement learning campaigns. We advocate the use of less accurate but more expeditious simulations, which might draw on data-driven, e.g., neural dynamics, models. This contribution argues that the practical value of a neural dynamics model for complex robot control lies in learning the \emph{right abstraction}: a reduced state that preserves the control-relevant physics of the high-fidelity system while enabling high-throughput policy learning. We develop a neural reduced dynamics (NRD) framework that separates the state the model propagates from what can be supplied as an input or recovered analytically, trains policies entirely inside the frozen learned model, and validates them back in the high-fidelity simulator. Two case studies instantiate it across three control tasks: terrain-aware HMMWV trajectory tracking on rigid, bumpy and deformable Continuum Representation Model (CRM) terrain; and goal reaching for a stock tracked vehicle and its front-mounted articulated arm. Every policy transfers back to the high-fidelity simulator. A single policy trained inside the terrain-conditioned dynamics model, and given no terrain input of its own, attains lower median and mean tracking error than both single-terrain specialists on all three terrains, including zero-shot bumpy terrain. Quantitatively, the tracked vehicle reaches 100 of 100 goals and the arm 97 of 100, with zero contacts or joint-limit violations. The NRD models advance roughly four orders of magnitude faster in simulated time than the high-fidelity simulator scenes they replace, making iterative on-policy learning practical and supporting neural reduced dynamics as a bridge between accurate but expensive physics simulation and scalable robot learning.

</details>


### [281] [SCAPE: Scenario-Conditioned Simulation-Augmented Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19425)
*Dijie Zhu,Seunghun Oh,Ruopeng Huang,Zhiyu Huang,Jiaqi Ma,Chen Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCAPE是一个场景条件化的模拟增强策略评估框架，通过利用有限的配对仿真-现实样本和大规模仿真回滚数据来预测场景条件下的真实世界策略性能，并校正仿真到现实的偏差，使用共形预测校准不确定性。在自动驾驶和四足机器人速度跟踪任务中验证了其有效性和样本效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的性能评估是机器人学习策略在现实世界部署的核心瓶颈。真实世界测试成本高且难以扩展，而纯仿真测试受sim-to-real差距影响存在偏差。现有模拟增强方法关注总体平均性能，掩盖了场景级别的性能差异，无法为策略的安放部署提供具体指导。

Method: SCAPE框架首先利用有限的配对仿真-现实样本校正仿真标签中的sim-to-real偏差，然后使用大规模仿真回滚数据训练场景条件化的预测模型，最后通过共形预测(conformal prediction)校准预测不确定性，从而预测场景条件下的真实世界策略性能。

Result: 在自动驾驶和四足速度跟踪任务中，SCAPE在sim-to-sim研究中相比场景条件化神经基线和聚合统计基线，场景级别预测误差分别降低4.9%/34.7%（驾驶）和14.5%/27.7%（四足）。在物理Unitree Go2上的实际部署评估中，SCAPE提升了测试样本效率，产生更窄的校准预测区间，对分布外场景有更好的泛化能力，并支持细粒度的部署策略。

Conclusion: SCAPE通过场景条件化的性能预测和偏差校正，克服了现有模拟增强评估方法只关注总体平均的局限，为策略的精细、安全部署提供了可靠的性能评估工具和建议。

Abstract: Reliable performance evaluation is a central bottleneck for deploying robot-learning policies in real-world conditions. Real-world testing is faithful but costly and difficult to scale, whereas simulation-based testing scales easily but is inevitably biased by the sim-to-real gap. Existing simulation-augmented methods combine limited real-world rollouts with abundant simulation proxies, but focus on performance averaged over initial conditions and deployment settings. Such population-level averages obscure scenario-specific variation and provide limited guidance about when and where a policy can be safely deployed. We propose SCAPE, a scenario-conditioned simulation-augmented policy evaluation framework that predicts scenario-conditioned real-world policy performance using limited paired sim-and-real samples and large-scale simulation rollouts. SCAPE corrects sim-to-real bias in simulation labels before training the prediction model and calibrates prediction uncertainty through conformal prediction. We validate SCAPE on autonomous driving and quadruped velocity tracking. In sim-to-sim studies, SCAPE reduces scenario-level prediction error by 4.9%/34.7% (driving) and 14.5%/27.7% (quadruped) relative to scene-conditioned neural and aggregate statistical baselines on average. We further evaluate a velocity-tracking policy deployed on a physical Unitree Go2. SCAPE also improves testing sample efficiency, produces narrower calibrated prediction intervals, generalizes better to out-of-distribution scenarios, and enables fine-grained deployment strategies.

</details>


### [282] [Hybrid Feedback Sampling for Sample-Efficient Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19443)
*Chaoyi Pan,Zeji Yi,John Zhang,Zachary Manchester,Guannan Qu,Guanya Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出反馈采样MPC（FS-MPC）算法，通过将采样与优化反馈策略结合，解决高维开环不稳定系统中采样型MPC因射击方法不稳定导致的样本效率低和数值不稳定问题。FS-MPC采用混合采样设计平衡局部与全局搜索，理论证明其收敛速度快于标准MPPI且优于标准反馈采样，实验验证其在多样接触丰富的控制任务及真实人形机器人上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样型MPC因并行性和灵活性被广泛用于真实机器人控制，但在高维、开环不稳定的动力系统中，改善控制序列所需样本数随视界呈指数增长，导致样本效率低下和数值不稳定。论文动机是揭示射击方法在采样型MPC中的不稳定性，并提出更优的采样方案。

Method: 论文首先证明最优采样建议分布可通过使用优化反馈策略进行采样来实现。基于此，提出FS-MPC算法，该算法采用混合采样设计，根据系统稳定性和可用计算预算在局部搜索与全局搜索之间取得平衡。通过理论分析证明混合采样方法相比标准MPPI具有更快的收敛速度，相比标准反馈采样具有更好的最优性。

Result: 在多样接触丰富的控制任务（如人形机器人定位操作和灵巧操作）中，FS-MPC成功处理了标准采样方法难以应对的动态不稳定任务，并严格优于仅使用反馈策略的方法。此外，该方法在真实世界的人形机器人运动和操作任务中得到验证。

Conclusion: FS-MPC通过混合采样策略有效结合局部与全局搜索，在保持采样型MPC灵活性的同时解决了其在高维不稳定系统中的样本效率与数值稳定性问题，并在仿真和真实机器人实验中均表现出优越性能。

Abstract: Thanks to its parallelizability and flexibility, sampling-based Model Predictive Control (MPC) has become widely popular for controlling real-world robotic systems. However, for high-dimensional and open-loop unstable dynamical systems, the required number of samples to improve the control sequence will grow exponentially with the horizon, leading to poor sample efficiency and numerical instability. This paper investigates the instability of shooting methods in sampling-based MPC and shows that the optimal sampling proposal distribution can be realized by sampling with an optimized feedback policy. We refer to this algorithm as Feedback Sampling MPC (FS-MPC). FS-MPC involves a hybrid sampling design which balances local and global search based on the system stability and the available computation budget. Our theoretical analysis shows that our hybrid sampling approach achieves faster convergence than standard MPPI and better optimality than standard feedback sampling. Empirically, in diverse contact-rich control tasks like humanoid loco-manipulation and dexterous manipulation, we show that FS-MPC successfully tackles dynamically unstable tasks where standard sample-based approaches struggle, and strictly outperforms feedback policies alone. Finally, we validate our method on humanoid robot locomotion and manipulation tasks in the real world.

</details>


### [283] [When Automata Meet Streams: Temporal Logic Compilation for Stream-Based Robotics Task and Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19453)
*Sayem Nazmuz Zaman,Cyrus Neary*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SAM-TD，一种将任意LTL_f时间约束编译进流式TAMP（任务与运动规划）的方法，支持规划过程中动态生成的流对象，并在三个PDDLStream环境和标准PDDL基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式TAMP求解器通常只推理目标可达性，无法满足长时域任务所需的时间规格（如安全关键的顺序、不变性和活性约束）。由于流在规划过程中通过迭代流细化循环生成不断扩展的几何对象集，导致现有时间逻辑编译技术无法直接应用，目前缺乏在流式求解器中强制实施时间约束的方法。

Method: SAM-TD将任意LTL_f约束转换为自动机，并将回归后的自动机守卫嵌入到动作模式中（在规划开始前预定义）。搜索过程中，SAM-TD同步更新自动机状态，并使用在所有自动机间共享的有效性令牌来剪枝违反约束的分支，从而无需枚举固定对象集或修改底层规划器。

Result: SAM-TD在三个机器人PDDLStream环境中首次实现了流式TAMP在LTL_f约束下的规划，并证明了其对规划过程中由流细化动态生成的对象支持；在标准离散PDDL基准上，与最先进的时间约束编译方法性能相当。

Conclusion: SAM-TD提供了一种高效且通用的编译方案，使流式TAMP能够满足任意LTL_f时间规格，填补了流式规划器缺乏时间约束支持的研究空白，并保持了与现有先进方法相当的竞争力。

Abstract: Stream-based robotics Task and Motion Planning (TAMP) integrates discrete symbolic planning with dynamically generated continuous geometric parameters, such as poses, grasps, and trajectories. However, stream-based planners typically reason only about goal reachability, whereas long-horizon tasks also demand adherence to temporal specifications, such as safety-critical ordering, invariance, and liveness constraints. No methods currently exist to enforce such temporal constraints for stream-based solvers because streams generate an expanding geometric object set via iterative stream refinement loops during planning, rendering existing temporal-logic compilation techniques incompatible. We therefore present Synchronous Action Monitoring with Token Destruction (SAM-TD), a compilation method that enforces arbitrary Linear Temporal Logic over finite traces ($\textrm{LTL}_f$) specifications in stream-based TAMP. SAM-TD translates arbitrary $\textrm{LTL}_f$ constraints into automata and embeds regressed automaton guards into action schemas, which are pre-specified before planning begins. By doing so, SAM-TD can handle objects generated by streams during planning, thus circumventing the need to enumerate a fixed object set or modify the underlying planner. During search, SAM-TD synchronously updates automaton states and uses a validity token shared across all automata to prune constraint-violating branches. We show that SAM-TD supports dynamically generated stream objects from iterative stream refinements during plan search. Experimental results provide the first ever demonstration of stream-based TAMP under $\textrm{LTL}_f$ constraints in three robotics PDDLStream environments. Furthermore, on standard discrete PDDL benchmarks, SAM-TD is competitive with state-of-the-art temporal-constraint compilation methods.

</details>


### [284] [Fine-Tuning VLAs with Self-Demonstrated Generative Control for Multi-Task Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19490)
*Prachi Garg,Steve Xing,Prahit Yaugand,Saurabh Gupta,Derek Hoiem*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种自监督方法，利用零样本VLA模型生成的在线交互rollouts作为额外训练数据来微调VLA模型，以解决在新机器人上部署时因硬件配置差异导致的性能下降问题，同时保持原有多任务能力和指令跟随能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最先进的VLA模型（如π0.5）在语义理解、指令跟随和行为任务上表现优异，但在部署到新机器人时，即使硬件配置存在微小差异也会导致严重性能下降。简单地在专家数据上微调虽然能提升特定任务表现，但会导致原有指令跟随能力丧失。

Method: 提出自监督方法：从零样本VLA模型生成在线交互rollouts作为额外的训练数据，与专家数据一起用于VLA模型的微调。

Result: 该微调方案在目标机器人上能产生强多任务策略：(1)保留从零样本模型蒸馏的先前任务；(2)保持通用指令跟随能力；(3)从专家数据中学习新技能且样本效率更高。在真实ALOHA机器人和RoboTwin新仿真基准上均验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 自监督生成的交互数据可以有效弥补零样本VLA与专家微调之间的权衡，实现既保留原有多任务先验又学习新技能的目标，为VLA在新机器人上的高效部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: State-of-the-art vision-language-action (VLA) models such as $π_{0.5}$ exhibit strong semantic understanding, instruction following and task behavior. However, when deployed on new robots, even minor mismatches in hardware configuration relative to pretraining can cause severe performance drops. Finetuning the VLA on in-domain expert data from the new embodiment improves performance on the expert task but leads to a loss in its original instruction following and behavioral priors. In this paper, we propose a self-supervised method that generates online interaction rollouts from the zero-shot VLA as additional training data for finetuning. Our experiments show this finetuning scheme yields strong multi-task policies that, on the target robot, (1) inherit prior tasks distilled from the zero-shot model, (2) enable generalist instruction following, while (3) learning new skills from expert data with improved sample efficiency. We demonstrate the success of our approach across test sets probing generalization on a real ALOHA robot and a new simulation benchmark in RoboTwin. Video results are available at https://self-supervised-control.pages.dev/

</details>


### [285] [LF-GICP: Parameter-Free Degeneracy-Aware LiDAR Odometry via a Voxel-Normal Localizability Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19522)
*Eunsoo Im*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种参数无关的退化处理框架LF-GICP，通过正则化无关的体素法向局部可定位场及其统计量（归一化分数f₀和逐体素质量λ₀）结合时间中值门控，触发Fisher信息对应加权，解决扫描到地图LiDAR里程计在几何退化环境（隧道、走廊）中的无界漂移问题，且无需环境特定调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR里程计在几何退化环境中沿不可观测轴无界漂移，而现有的退化处理方法依赖环境特定参数调优，缺乏泛化性。作者还发现体素化GICP中GN海森矩阵掩盖了平移退化，因为协方差正则化使平移块人为保持良态，这阻碍了退化检测。

Method: (1) 提出正则化无关的体素法向局部可定位场取代协方差正则化；(2) 利用两个统计量：归一化分数f₀检测方向各向异性，绝对逐体素质量λ₀区分信息缺失（隧道）与信息稀释（密集开阔场景）；(3) 时间中值门控结合两者触发Fisher信息对应加权；(4) 通过固定规则在两个短序列上校准一次后冻结。

Result: 在KITTI上达到最低相对平移误差0.865%（与重跑的基线在相同评估协议下对比）；在GEODE隧道和MulRan数据集上优于基线；在HeLiPR上均值领先；无需重新调参即可泛化到四种传感器类型。

Conclusion: LF-GICP提供了一种无需环境特定调参的通用退化处理方案，并在多个基准上展现领先性能；同时实证表明直的均匀隧道沿其轴向对纯LiDAR配准保持不可观测。

Abstract: Scan-to-map LiDAR odometry drifts unboundedly along the unobservable axes of geometrically degenerate environments like tunnels and corridors, and existing degeneracy handling requires environment-specific parameter tuning. This paper presents a parameter-free approach. We show that in voxelized GICP the Gauss--Newton (GN) Hessian masks translational degeneracy, because covariance regularization keeps the translation block artificially well-conditioned. We bypass this with a regularization-free voxel-normal localizability field and two of its statistics: a normalized fraction $f_0$ detecting directional anisotropy, and an absolute per-voxel mass $λ_0$ distinguishing information absence (tunnels) from dilution (dense open scenes). A temporal-median gate combines both to trigger Fisher-information correspondence weighting. Calibrated once by fixed rules on two short sequences and then frozen, LF-GICP achieves the lowest KITTI relative translation error ($0.865\%$) under an identical evaluation protocol against re-run baselines, outperforms them on GEODE tunnels and MulRan, leads the HeLiPR mean, and generalizes across four sensor types without re-tuning. We further demonstrate empirically that straight, uniform tunnels remain unobservable along their axis for LiDAR-only registration.

</details>


### [286] [Multimodal Trajectory Planning for Surface Vehicles using Turning Circle-based Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19537)
*Changyu Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种无引导路径的多模态轨迹规划框架，将模型预测控制（MPC）与基于转向圆的控制障碍函数（TC-CBF）相结合，通过嵌入规避方向来探索拓扑不同的轨迹，解决自主水面船舶在动态环境中的局部最小值和死锁问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于欧氏距离的控制障碍函数（ED-CBF）仅根据邻近度评估安全性，忽略了水面船舶的非完整运动学和有限转向能力，导致单模MPC面临局部最小值与死锁问题；而现有多种多模态规划方法依赖全局引导路径，灵活性和计算效率受限。

Method: 将MPC与TC-CBF集成：TC-CBF利用船舶转向圆的几何特性构建可行避碰区域，生成左转和右转两种分布式规避模式，并将规避方向直接嵌入安全约束中，使优化求解器能够在无需全局引导路径的情况下探索并选择拓扑不同的轨迹。

Result: 在涉及多个移动船舶的广泛仿真中，与单模基线相比，所提方法在各类交通密度下均实现了更高的任务成功率、更少的安全违规次数以及更小的残余违规量。

Conclusion: 通过TC-CBF嵌入规避方向的多模态MPC框架，有效缓解了单模MPC的局部最小值和死锁问题，同时保持了计算效率，为水上自主航行提供了一种无需引导路径的可靠避碰方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a guide path-free multimodal trajectory planning framework for autonomous surface vehicles operating in dynamic environments. The proposed method integrates model predictive control (MPC) with a turning circle-based control barrier function (TC-CBF). Unlike conventional Euclidean distance-based CBFs (ED-CBFs), which evaluate safety solely based on proximity, the TC-CBF accounts for the nonholonomic motion and finite turning capability of a surface vehicle. Its geometric formulation identifies feasible avoidance regions according to the vehicle's turning circles and generates distinct left- and right-turning avoidance modes. These modes allow the optimization solver to explore and select topologically different trajectories without relying on globally planned guide paths, as required by many conventional multimodal planning approaches. By embedding the avoidance direction directly into the safety constraint, the proposed framework alleviates the local-minimum and deadlock problems of single-mode MPC while maintaining computational efficiency. Extensive simulations involving multiple moving vessels demonstrate that the proposed method achieves higher success rates, fewer safety violations, and smaller residual violations than single-mode baselines across all tested traffic densities.

</details>


### [287] [HiTac-WAM: A Hierarchical Tactile World Action Model for Contact-Rich Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19574)
*Chao Xue,Chaofan Zhang,Wenxuan Ma,Guocai Yao,Shaowei Cui,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HiTac-WAM是一个分层触觉世界动作模型，通过预测候选动作块的接触状态、3D形变场和滑移风险这三个按层次组织的触觉状态序列来指导机器人规划和执行，在真实机器人任务中将成功率从31.1%提升至72.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型能联合预测未来视觉观测和动作，但触觉感知的世界模型通常将未来触觉表示为图像或潜变量流，缺乏对触觉状态之间物理依赖关系的建模。触觉状态本质上是分层的（接触→形变→滑移），这种结构关系未被现有方法利用。

Method: HiTac-WAM提出分层触觉世界动作模型：(1) 预测分解为接触状态、3D形变场和滑移风险三个层级，形成有向层次结构，每一下游阶段以上游阶段的停止梯度信号为条件；(2) 采用有向注意力掩码，使触觉查询能够关注每个候选动作的视频-动作上下文，同时阻止视频和动作查询关注触觉token；(3) 规划时根据触觉预测和任务进度估计对候选动作块排序；(4) 执行时保留所选触觉预测作为参考，当预测与观测的触觉状态出现持续偏差时触发纠错性重新规划。

Result: HiTac-WAM在接触F1分数上达到0.921；在匹配训练预算下，有向层次结构相比仅形变预测器将3D位移L2误差降低17.6%，相比仅滑移预测器将滑移AUPRC提升60.4%。在芯片抓取、黑板擦拭和USB插入任务中，层次化预测引导的选择将真实机器人平均成功率从31.1%提高到61.1%，完整系统达到72.2%。

Conclusion: HiTac-WAM表明，通过显式建模触觉状态间的物理层次依赖关系（接触→形变→滑移），并利用有向注意力机制实现触觉对未来动作的预测及执行中的纠错重规划，能够显著提升机器人触觉操作任务的性能。该工作验证了分层触觉预测在真实机器人世界模型中的有效性。

Abstract: World action models jointly predict future visual observations and actions, whereas existing tactile-aware variants typically represent future touch as an image or latent stream without modeling the physical dependencies that organize tactile states hierarchically. We present HiTac-WAM, a hierarchical tactile world action model that forecasts a sequence of future tactile states for each candidate action chunk before execution. The forecast factorizes into contact state, a 3D deformation field, and slip risk, organized as a directed hierarchy in which each downstream stage is conditioned on stop-gradient signals from preceding stages. A directed attention mask allows tactile queries to attend to the video-action context of each candidate while preventing video and action queries from attending to tactile tokens. For planning, HiTac-WAM ranks candidate action chunks using tactile forecasts and task-progress estimates. For execution, the selected tactile forecast is retained as a reference; persistent discrepancies between predicted and observed tactile states trigger corrective replanning. HiTac-WAM achieves a mean contact F1 of 0.921; under matched training budgets, the directed hierarchy reduces 3D displacement L2 error by 17.6% relative to the deformation-only predictor and improves slip AUPRC by 60.4% relative to the slip-only predictor. Across chip grasping, blackboard erasing, and USB insertion, selection guided by the hierarchical forecasts increases the average real-robot success rate from 31.1% to 61.1%, while the full system attains 72.2%.

</details>


### [288] [OrthoSkillVLA: Continual Skill Learning via Gradient-Informed Skill Subspace Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19589)
*Jiaqi Wang,Zhou Fang,Qiongfeng Shi,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出OrthoSkillVLA，一种无需演示回放的参数高效框架，用于预训练VLA模型的持续技能学习。它针对VLM和ActionHead分别施加子空间约束，并为输出层引入轻量级特征感知MoE解码器，在模拟和真实环境中均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练VLA模型在顺序适应多样化技能时，会扰动先前技能使用的表示和速度映射，导致灾难性遗忘。现有架构方法隔离技能但增加推理开销，子空间约束方法对整个模型施加统一约束，忽略了VLA内部组件（VLM和ActionHead）的不同角色。此外，最终速度解码器作为读出层存在冻结瓶颈与更新覆盖的两难问题。

Method: OrthoSkillVLA包含三个核心设计：(1) 鉴于VLM维护广泛语义表示、ActionHead将语义细化为局部速度模式，对两者分别施加不同的子空间约束，以保留可复用语义容量并保护局部速度模式；(2) 引入轻量级特征感知MoE解码器，每个技能分配一个紧凑专家，训练无关的路由器根据特征空间亲和度选择专家，解决输出层表达瓶颈与速度映射覆盖的困境；(3) 整体框架为参数高效，无需演示回放。

Result: 在大量模拟和真实世界实验以及消融研究中，OrthoSkillVLA在保留先前技能和获取新技能两方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过组件感知的子空间约束和特征感知MoE解码器，OrthoSkillVLA有效解决了VLA持续技能学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，证明了区分VLA内部组件异质性对于持续学习的重要性。

Abstract: Pretrained Vision-Language-Action models provide a strong foundation for robot learning, but sequentially adapting them to diverse skills can perturb the representations and velocity mappings used by previous skills, leading to catastrophic forgetting. Architecture-based approaches improve retention by isolating skills but lead to increased inference footprint. Recent subspace-constrained methods restrict parameter updates in an orthogonal subspace to minimize interference but impose a unified constraint on the entire model. We analyze the distinct roles of internal VLA components and identify two VLA-specific challenges. First, the VLM maintains broad semantic representations, making it vulnerable to capacity exhaustion, whereas the ActionHead refines semantics into localized velocity patterns that are highly sensitive to perturbations. Second, the final velocity decoder serves as a readout layer. Freezing it forms an output-stage expressivity bottleneck, while updating it risks overwriting previous velocity mappings. To this end, we propose OrthoSkillVLA, a parameter-efficient framework for continual skill learning in pretrained VLA models without demonstration replay. Given the representation heterogeneity, we impose separate subspace constraints on the VLM and ActionHead, preserving reusable semantic capacity while protecting localized velocity patterns. For the output layer, we introduce a lightweight feature-aware MoE decoder, where each skill is allocated a compact expert and a training-free router selects the expert according to feature-space affinity. Extensive simulated and real-world evaluations, together with ablations, demonstrate that OrthoSkillVLA better preserves prior skills while acquiring new ones.

</details>


### [289] [The Verification Gap in Networked Physical AI: A Post-Semantic Communication Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19593)
*Shunsuke Saruwatari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向网络化物理AI的后语义通信框架（Post-Semantic Communication Framework），解决从任务提案到物理执行之间的"验证鸿沟"（verification gap）——即提案有效但缺乏证据或授权而无法最终执行的问题。框架将证据要求、证据记录、证据充分性与授权最终化及运行时门控分离，并区分证据传递（evidence transfer）与证据协调（evidence coordination），通过有限状态机制验证求值器的一致性。受控通信研究表明最终化端点（发送端vs接收端）决定反馈机制的不对称性：发送端最终化的反馈通过证据传递扩展可达性，接收端最终化的反馈通过协调抑制冗余传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在物理AI网络中，一个任务有效的提案并不必然是可执行的物理行动。提案可能被理解且有效，但支持该提案的证据或最终执行行动所需的授权可能不可用。这种"提案有效性"与"物理可执行性"之间的不匹配被定义为"验证鸿沟"，现有通信框架未系统处理这一接口问题。

Method: 提出后语义通信框架，核心要素包括：(1) 应用声明的证据需求；(2) 将合格观察表示为证据记录；(3) 通过单一路径验证支持性和冲突性记录；(4) 将证据充分性（evidence sufficiency）与授权最终化（authorized finalization）及下游运行时门控分离；(5) 区分证据传递（扩大最终化端点可达记录集）与证据协调（抑制已在端点持有的记录的传输）。采用有限状态机框架检查验证求值器对声明的区分实现的一致性，并通过受控通信研究分析不同最终化端点下的行为。

Result: 有限状态框架检验确认求值器能一致地实现声明的区分。受控通信研究揭示最终化端点依赖的不对称性：发送端最终化的反馈（Feedback）通过证据传递在整个可行绘制区域内扩展证据可达性；而接收端最终化的反馈则通过证据协调抑制冗余有效载荷，直到丢包、延迟、新鲜度和截止时间成本使选择转向单向通信（One-way）。此外提出端到端（episode-level）报告模式，为未来可测量的物理AI研究定义共同基准。

Conclusion: 该框架为物理AI系统中"提案形成"与"物理执行"之间的接口提供了形式化的通信与验证机制，明确了证据充分性与授权最终化的分离原则，并揭示了最终化端点选择对通信策略的深远影响。所提出的报告模式有望促进物理AI研究中可复现、可比较的评估标准。

Abstract: A task-effective proposal is not yet a justified physical action. In networked Physical AI, a proposal may be understood while valid, timely, proposal-bound evidence or the authority required to finalize an action remains unavailable. We call this mismatch the verification gap and introduce a Post-Semantic Communication Framework for the systems interface between proposal formation and physical execution. The framework begins with application-declared evidence requirements, represents qualifying observations as evidence records, validates supporting and conflicting records through one path, and separates evidence sufficiency from authorized finalization and a downstream runtime gate. It further distinguishes evidence transfer, which can enlarge the record set reachable by a finalizer, from evidence coordination, which can suppress transmission around records already held at the finalization endpoint. Finite-state framework checks verify that the evaluator implements the declared distinctions consistently. Under the declared model, the controlled communication study exposes a finalizer-dependent asymmetry: sender-finalized Feedback uses evidence transfer to expand evidence reachability throughout the feasible plotted region, whereas receiver-finalized Feedback uses coordination to suppress redundant payload until loss, latency, freshness, and deadline costs shift selection to One-way. Finally, an episode-level reporting schema defines common denominators for future measured Physical-AI studies.

</details>


### [290] [What Matters for Latent Actions in Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19613)
*Xizhou Bu,Qingda Hu,Lei Zhou,Lingfeng Zhang,Yingbo Tang,Zihao Liu,Xinyi Tao,Zhiqiang Ma,Qingqiu Huang,Chufeng Tang,Hongbo Wang,Jing Zhang,Jiayi Ma,Hangjun Ye,Wei Li,Xiaoshuai Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次对潜在动作模型（LAM）在机器人操作中的应用进行了全面的实证研究，在统一的自编码框架下系统比较了41种LAM设计选择，涵盖建模范式、学习目标与正则化方法、潜在动作集成策略三个维度，并通过四个代理指标评估其与下游操作性能的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LAM已成为利用大规模无标签视频学习机器人操作的有力范式，但该领域研究高度碎片化，各方法在隔离且不一致的实验条件下评估不同设计选择，难以辨别真正决定下游机器人操作性能的关键因素。

Method: 将代表性LAM方法统一到公共自编码框架中，系统研究41种LAM设计选择，涵盖三个维度：潜在动作建模范式、学习目标与正则化方法、潜在动作集成策略。同时考察四个代理指标评估潜在动作质量，并在三个广泛使用的基准及真实机器人操作任务上进行实验。

Result: 广泛的实验提供了强有力的经验证据，表明使用潜在动作微调VLM骨干网络为下游策略学习提供了更强的初始化，该结论在真实机器人操作任务中得到了进一步验证。

Conclusion: 通过对LAM设计空间的系统解构与实证比较，本文为潜在动作学习在机器人操作领域提供了首个全面基准，明确了VLM骨干微调作为关键设计要素，有助于推动该领域研究走向系统化和可比较的方向。

Abstract: Latent Action Models (LAMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for enabling robot learning to leverage large-scale unlabeled videos through latent actions that serve as compact surrogates for physical actions. Despite rapid progress, research on LAM remains highly fragmented, with existing methods evaluating different design choices in isolation under inconsistent experimental settings, making it difficult to identify the factors that truly determine downstream robotic manipulation performance. In this work, we present the first comprehensive empirical study of latent action learning for robotic manipulation. We unify representative LAM methods within a common autoencoding framework and systematically investigate 41 LAM design choices across three dimensions, including latent action modeling paradigms, learning objectives and regularization methods, and latent action integration strategies. We further examine four proxy metrics for evaluating latent action quality and assess their ability to reliably predict downstream robotic manipulation performance. Extensive experiments on three widely used benchmarks provide strong empirical evidence that fine-tuning vision-language model (VLM) backbones with latent actions provides a stronger initialization for downstream policy learning, with further validation on real-world robot manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [291] [Magnetically Self-Sealed MR Haptic Actuator With PWM-Based Excitation and High-Fidelity Torque Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19635)
*Dong Qiang,Tian Yuan,Song Yang,Kequan Xia,Thomas Reddyhoff,Yikun Zhang,Cheng Cheng,Min Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种集成式磁流变液（MRF）触觉系统，包括紧凑型磁自密封旋转执行器、低迟滞PWM驱动、基于模型的高保真扭矩渲染以及长时间稳定运行能力。实验表明10 kHz PWM频率下迟滞最小，所提出的混合控制器（前馈+迟滞补偿+PI+滑模修正）相比PID将方波过冲、下冲和稳态RMSE分别降低77.4%、61.9%和68.3%，并在1.5小时连续运行测试中仅温升2.5°C且无跟踪丢失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于安全、可感知的人机交互，准确且稳定的扭矩渲染至关重要。MRF执行器虽具有紧凑和快速可控的优势，但实际应用中需要解决流体密封、低迟滞激励、精确扭矩控制和长时间稳定运行四个关键挑战。

Method: 通过磁静态仿真引导磁性和非磁性材料布局以聚焦磁通于多盘扭矩区和永磁密封区；采用PWM驱动并研究频率对迟滞的影响；建立随扭矩变化方向与速度变化的非线性模型；设计结合前馈、迟滞补偿、PI反馈和滑模修正的实时控制器。

Result: 最大输出达600 N·mm/A；10 kHz PWM下迟滞最小且可重复性最佳；所提控制器相比PID大幅降低方波过冲（77.4%）、下冲（61.9%）和稳态RMSE（68.3%）；可跟踪正弦和生物力学模型参考信号；1.5小时长时间测试线圈附近仅温升2.5°C且无跟踪退化。

Conclusion: 该高保真扭矩渲染系统将从根本上变革人机协作，使交互更安全、更高效、更直观。

Abstract: Accurate and stable torque rendering is essential for safe and perceptive human--machine interaction. Magnetorheological fluid (MRF)-based actuators offer a compact and rapidly controllable solution for haptic feedback, but their practical implementation requires reliable fluid sealing, low-hysteresis excitation, accurate torque control, and stable long-duration operation. This article presents an integrated MRF haptic system featuring a compact magnetically self-sealed rotary actuator, low-hysteresis PWM operation, high-fidelity model-based torque rendering, and stable performance during long-time operation. Magnetostatic simulation guides the arrangement of magnetic and nonmagnetic materials to focus flux in the multidisk torque and permanent-magnet sealing regions, enabling a maximum 600 N$\cdot$mm/A output. Experiments show that higher PWM frequencies reduce hysteresis and improve repeatability. At 10 kHz, the response is represented by a nonlinear model that varies with the direction and speed of torque change. The real-time controller combines feedforward, hysteresis compensation, PI feedback, and sliding-mode correction. Compared with PID, it reduces square-wave overshoot, undershoot, and steady-state RMSE by 77.4\%, 61.9\%, and 68.3\%, respectively. It tracks sinusoidal and biomechanics-model-based references, and a 1.5-h test shows only a 2.5 $^\circ$C rise near the coil with no clear tracking loss. This high-fidelity torque rendering will fundamentally transform human--robot collaboration by making interactions safer, more efficient, and more intuitive.

</details>


### [292] [World-Model-Grounded LLM Planning for AUV and ASV Navigation Near Offshore Wind Farms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19661)
*Markus Buchholz,Ignacio Carlucho,Yvan R. Petillot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用物理世界模型增强大语言模型（LLM）驱动的机器人规划器，通过物理感知的神经世界模型、三阶段梯度轨迹优化器和MPC式闭环重规划器，将LLM的"做什么"决策与"做多久"的物理判断解耦，在海洋机器人（6-DOF AUV和3-DOF ASV）的基准任务中实现零碰撞和显著降低的GazeboSim目标距离误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽然能将自然语言指令转化为机器人动作序列，但缺乏物理感知能力，无法判断指令应持续多长时间或是否会导致机器人漂移撞上障碍物。这一缺陷限制了LLM在物理世界中的实际部署。

Method: 方法包含三个核心组件：(1) 物理感知的神经世界模型；(2) 三阶段基于梯度的轨迹优化器；(3) 带信任域保护的MPC式闭环重规划器。LLM负责语义决策（做什么），世界模型负责物理判断（做多久）。此外还提出了VLM辅助的语义映射管线，可从卫星图像、航海图和天气预报API提取障碍物信息，替代手工标注的障碍物几何。

Result: 在两种海洋平台（6-DOF AUV和3-DOF ASV）上各进行五个基准任务，两种平台均到达所有目标点且预测碰撞为零。在GazeboSim的海洋洋流、波浪和推进器动力学下保持无碰撞，相较未加物理基础的基线，ASV目标距离误差降低70-82%，AUV降低约93%。残差微调后代理滚动均方根误差分别降低60%（AUV）和69%（ASV）。ASV的VLM语义映射管线达到96%的可导航性准确率。

Conclusion: 世界模型能有效弥补LLM在物理时序和约束判断上的不足，实现LLM语义规划与物理执行的无缝衔接。该方法在海洋机器人平台上验证了可行性，VLM辅助的语义映射进一步降低了对外部精确几何信息的依赖，为LLM驱动的自主系统提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models can turn a natural-language mission into a sequence of robot actions, but they do not have a sense of physics: they cannot judge how long a command should run, or whether it will make the robot drift into an obstacle. We proposed the use of a world model to expand the capabilities of Large Language model-based planners. Our method has three components: a physics-grounded neural world model, a three-phase gradient-based trajectory optimizer, and a Model Predictive Controller (MPC)-style closed-loop replanner with a trust-region guard. The language model decides what to do, and the world model decides how long, whether that means driving eight thrusters through 6 DOF or two differential thrusters through 3 DOF. We evaluate two marine vehicle classes operating near offshore wind infrastructure: a 6-DOF Autonomous Underwater Vehicle (AUV) and a 3-DOF differential-drive Autonomous Surface Vehicle (ASV). In five benchmark missions per platform, both vehicles reach every goal with zero predicted collisions, and both transfer to GazeboSim under ocean current, waves, and thruster dynamics, remaining collision-free and cutting GazeboSim goal-distance error versus the ungrounded baseline by 70-82% (ASV) and roughly 93% (AUV), after a residual fine-tuning pass that separately reduces surrogate rollout Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) by 60% (AUV) and 69% (ASV). For the ASV we further demonstrate a Vision language model (VLM)-assisted semantic-mapping pipeline that extracts obstacles and environmental context from satellite imagery, nautical charts, and forecast Application Programming Interface (API) instead of onboard sensors, reaching 96% navigability accuracy as a drop-in replacement for hand-specified obstacle geometry.

</details>


### [293] [SAGE: Ergodic Control for Autonomous and Adaptive Inspection of Subsea Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19671)
*Markus Buchholz,Ignacio Carlucho,Yvan R. Petillot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE（语义与自适应生成遍历性控制）架构，用于水下采油树（XTs）阀门巡检。相比传统固定路径A*规划，SAGE通过遍历控制让水下机器人按实时传感器推导的风险分布分配巡检时间，而非预定路线。仿真（两棵采油树、五个阀门）表明，遍历控制可将高风险阀门的再次巡检间隔从A*的固定8.1秒缩短至5.8秒，且新检测到的泄漏可在下一个控制周期即刻改变机器人行为，无需重规划或人工介入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并非所有水下采油树阀门在同一时刻承担相同风险：疑似泄漏的阀门需要比历史正常的阀门被更频繁地检查，且风险画像会在任务过程中随新泄漏发现而变化。传统脚本式路线方法无法动态适配这种实时变化的风险分布。

Method: 提出SAGE（Semantic and Adaptive Generative Ergodicity）架构，基于遍历控制（ergodic control）原理，使机器人巡检时间分配与实时传感器推导出的风险分布成正比。风险分布在每个控制周期被重新计算而非规划一次，新检测到的泄漏无需显式重规划步骤即可影响下一控制周期的行为。论文推导了该遍历控制律，并在五阀门（两棵采油树）仿真场景中与固定循环A*路线进行对比。

Result: 仿真结果表明：两种方法可被调优到在高风险阀门附近花费相似的总时间，但只有遍历控制能做到更频繁地检查该阀门——在主导性风险阀门上，遍历控制的再次巡检间隔为5.8秒，而A*对所有阀门固定为8.1秒（无论风险高低），意味着泄漏可能被发现的等待时间缩短了约三分之一。

Conclusion: SAGE架构在动态风险环境下优于固定路线方法：不仅降低了对高风险阀门的巡检延迟，还实现了无需重规划和人工介入的实时自适应行为调整，验证了遍历控制在海底油气设施巡检中的有效性。

Abstract: Subsea Christmas Trees (XTs) are underwater structures that use valves for directing oil flow, needing constant inspection. But not every valve carries the same risk at the same time: a valve with a suspected leak needs to be revisited far more often than one with a clean history, and that risk picture changes during the mission as new leaks are found. To handle this, we present SAGE (Semantic and Adaptive Generative Ergodicity), an ergodic-control architecture that allocates vehicle time in proportion to a live, sensor-derived risk distribution rather than a scripted route. We study a two-XT scenario, with five valves in total, and compare a fixed-loop A* tour against SAGE. Both methods can be tuned to spend similar total time near a high-risk valve, but only ergodic control also checks it more often: in simulation, a dominant-risk valve was revisited every 5.8 s under ergodic control against a fixed 8.1 s for every valve under A*, regardless of risk, so a leak can go unnoticed for barely two-thirds as long. Because the tracked distribution is recomputed rather than planned once, a newly detected leak shifts vehicle behavior on the next control cycle with no explicit re-planning step and no operator in the loop, which a fixed tour cannot do without a discrete re-route. We derive the ergodic control law behind this behavior and report simulation results on the five-valve scenario.

</details>


### [294] [Keeping the Franka Emika Panda alive: a ROS 2 stack with a reliable position interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19740)
*Antonio Langella,Davide Risi,Vincenzo Petrone,Enrico Ferrentino,Pasquale Chiacchio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个开源软件栈，恢复并增强了Franka Emika Panda机器人的ROS 2支持。作者首先分析了外部位置控制接口不稳定的根本原因，指出振动和保护性停机源于外部控制环的时序与采样抖动而非机器人本身的限制。基于此分析，引入了异步硬件接口、速率匹配机制和位置域参考生成策略，实验验证该架构能可靠地跟踪速度参考并减少官方实现引入的运动伪影，在规划、柔顺控制、位置控制操作和触觉遥操作等多个场景中均得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Franka Emika Panda机器人原有的ROS 2支持存在长期未解决的可靠性问题，其外部位置控制接口不稳定，导致振动和保护性停机，这严重阻碍了基于Panda的安全、响应式人机协作应用开发。

Method: 首先对不稳定位置控制进行根因分析，证明问题源于控制环时序和采样抖动；然后提出三种关键技术：1）异步硬件接口，将实时通信与ROS 2控制环解耦；2）速率匹配机制，适配较慢的命令源；3）位置域参考生成策略，产生平滑可靠的位置指令。

Result: 实验验证表明所提出架构能可靠跟踪速度参考，相比官方实现显著减少运动伪影；该软件栈在运动规划、柔顺控制、位置控制操作和触觉遥操作四个任务场景中，在两个独立的Panda平台上均得到验证。

Conclusion: 该工作通过恢复现代、可靠、开源的Panda ROS 2生态系统，降低了开发集成规划、感知、交互和共享自主的HRI应用的准入门槛，有助于构建安全、可复现的人机协作系统。

Abstract: This paper presents an open-source software stack that restores ROS 2 support for the Franka Emika Panda robot while resolving the long-standing unreliability of its external position control interface. We first analyze the root causes of unstable position control and show that the observed vibrations and protective stops arise from the timing of the external control loop and sampling jitter, rather than from limitations of the robot itself. Building on this analysis, we introduce an asynchronous hardware interface that decouples real-time communication from the ROS 2 control loop, a rate-matching mechanism for slower command sources, and a position-domain reference generation strategy that produces reliable, smooth position commands. Experimental validation shows that the proposed architecture reliably tracks velocity references by reducing motion artifacts introduced by the official implementation, and the stack is validated across motion planning, compliance control, position-controlled manipulation, and haptic teleoperation on two independent Panda platforms. By restoring a modern, reliable, and open ROS 2 ecosystem for the Panda, this work lowers the barrier to developing safe, responsive, and reproducible human-robot collaboration applications that integrate planning, perception, interaction, and shared autonomy. Code and videos are available on our website at https://sites.google.com/view/fer-ros2/.

</details>


### [295] [GOAG: Generative and Object-Agnostic Grasp Planner for Dexterous Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19759)
*Julien Merand,Boris Meden,Mathieu Grossard,Liming Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GOAG是一种基于生成式模型的多指机器人抓取规划方法，其核心洞察是抓取器与物体在接触点处共享相同的表面几何特征。该方法学习抓取器的接触表面分布，仅在推理时引入物体特征，实现了物体无关的泛化抓取规划，在MultiDex数据集上达到86.93%的平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指抓取是关键的机器人技能，但现有基于深度学习的抓取规划器通常依赖物体特定的数据集进行训练，导致在面对新物体时泛化能力不足。

Method: 提出GOAG（Generative and Object-Agnostic Grasp Planner），一种深度生成模型。该方法基于"抓取器与物体在相互接触点处具有相同表面几何"的观察，学习特定抓取器接触表面分布的紧凑潜表示，从而无需物体特定训练数据即可高效采样有效的抓取配置。物体特征仅在推理时引入，以检索与抓取器能力兼容的可接受接触区域。

Result: 在模拟和真实场景的多种抓取协议上进行了广泛验证，在MultiDex数据集上达到86.93%的平均成功率，达到最先进水平。与专门在该数据集上训练的方法性能相当，但处理速度更快，且不依赖物体特定的训练数据。

Conclusion: GOAG有效解决了传统数据驱动抓取规划器的泛化挑战，证明了物体无关学习在灵巧操作中的优势，并已开源代码和视频。

Abstract: Multifingered grasping is a crucial robotic skill, but current deep-learning grasp planners often struggle to generalize to new objects because they are trained on limited, object-specific datasets. We introduce a fundamentally different approach, grounded in the observation that the gripper and the object share identical surface geometry at their mutual contact points. We propose GOAG: Generative and Object-Agnostic Grasp Planner for Dexterous Robotic Manipulation, a novel deep generative model that learns a compact latent representation of a specific gripper's contact surface distribution, enabling the efficient sampling of valid grasp configurations without relying on object-specific training data. We show that by introducing object features only at inference time, our model can effectively retrieve admissible contact areas that are compatible with the gripper's capabilities. We validate our approach through extensive experiments on established grasp protocols in both simulated and real-world scenarios, demonstrating its effectiveness with different grippers from the literature. Our method delivers state-of-the-art results on the objects from the MultiDex dataset, achieving an average success rate of 86.93%. It offers significantly faster processing when generating numerous grasps, while matching the performance of leading approaches specifically trained on this dataset. Unlike these methods, our approach does not rely on object-specific training data, highlighting the advantages of object-agnostic learning. It effectively addresses the generalization challenges faced by traditional data-driven grasp planners. Code and videos are available on our project website https://cea-list.github.io/goagweb/ .

</details>


### [296] [CoToGrasp: Contact-Topology-Conditioned Dexterous Grasp Synthesis via Canonical Workspace Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19776)
*Julien Merand,Boris Meden,Liming Chen,Mathieu Grossard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CoToGrasp，一种以特定接触拓扑为条件、可零样本泛化到未见物体的生成式灵巧抓取框架，通过特征化规范工作空间实现目标无关训练，并在DexGraspNet数据集及物理机器人平台上验证了SOTA性能与物理可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧抓取规划主要优化物理稳定性，忽略了抓取方式对下游功能任务的支持；而基于人类抓取分类学进行条件化抓取合成又需要昂贵且逐物体标注的数据集，存在数据采集瓶颈。

Method: 提出CoToGrasp生成式框架，严格以特定接触拓扑为条件合成多样且稳定的抓取。采用特征化规范工作空间，将局部物体特征投影到统一的夹爪中心域，从而在物体语义功能意图与任意几何形状之间解耦；在该工作空间内学习夹爪的内在接触流形，实现训练目标无关，推理时对未见物体零样本泛化。

Result: 在大型DexGraspNet数据集上的广泛评估表明CoToGrasp取得了最先进性能，优于现有分类学引导的规划器；并在物理机器人平台上展示了合成接触拓扑的物理可行性与运动学可行性。

Conclusion: 通过将接触拓扑作为条件并经特征化规范工作空间实现物体无关训练，CoToGrasp有效绕过了数据标注瓶颈，实现了零样本泛化的功能化灵巧抓取合成，展现了实际应用的潜力。

Abstract: Current dexterous grasp planners primarily optimize for physical stability, focusing on whether an object can be grasped rather than how it should be grasped to support downstream functional tasks. However, conditioning grasp synthesis on specific human grasp taxonomies typically requires prohibitively expensive, object-annotated datasets. To address these limitations, we propose CoToGrasp, a novel generative framework that synthesizes diverse, stable grasps strictly conditioned on specific contact topologies. To bypass the data collection bottleneck, CoToGrasp is trained entirely in an object-agnostic manner. We introduce a feature-based canonical workspace that projects local object features into a unified gripper-centric domain, effectively decoupling the semantic functional intent from the arbitrary object geometry. By learning the intrinsic contact manifold of the gripper within this workspace, our model achieves zero-shot generalization to unseen objects at inference. Extensive evaluations on the large-scale DexGraspNet dataset demonstrate that CoToGrasp achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming existing taxonomy-guided planners. Finally, we demonstrate the physical viability and kinematic feasibility of our synthesized contact topologies on a physical robot platform. Code is available on our project website https://cea-list.github.io/cotograspweb/ .

</details>


### [297] [MILD: Tractable Terrain Modeling for Learning Improved Bipedal Locomotion on Deformable Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19955)
*Zeren Luo,Jiahui Zhang,Zhe Xu,Wanyue Li,Xinqi Li,Xuechao Chen,Zhangguo Yu,Annan Tang,Peng Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MILD系统，通过物理基础的离散元接触求解器准确模拟可变形地面，并训练具备潜在调制和本体感觉估计的地形感知运动控制器，使双足机器人在软质地形上实现自然适应和在线地形识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双足机器人在可变形地面上的运动能力受限，因为现有模拟器无法捕捉屈服基底中的时空异质性，导致训练出的控制器在真实软质地面上适应能力不足。

Method: 提出MILD，包含两个核心组件：(1) 基于物理的离散元接触求解器，准确模拟空间变化的足部-地形交互；(2) 基于深度强化学习训练的地形感知运动控制器，采用潜在调制和本体感觉估计实现实时地形识别与适应。

Result: 与最先进方法相比，MILD在训练中生成更多样且真实的接触场景，所得的控制器在真实可变形表面上表现出自然适应能力。硬件实验验证了系统在广泛表面刚度范围内的在线地形识别和适应能力。

Conclusion: 通过物理真实的地形建模与学习型控制的结合，MILD有效缩小了仿真与真实可变形地形之间的差距，为双足机器人在屈服地形上的可靠运动提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Enabling robots to walk on yielding terrain is vital for applications ranging from disaster response to planetary exploration. While bipedal robots hold immense potential, their locomotion on deformable surfaces remains limited as current simulators fail to capture the spatiotemporal heterogeneity of such yielding substrates. We present MILD, featuring a physics-grounded discrete-element contact solver that accurately simulates spatially varying foot-terrain interactions. Complementing this model, we train a terrain-aware locomotion controller via deep reinforcement learning with latent modulation and proprioceptive estimation. Quantitative comparisons against state-of-the-art methods show our approach generates more diverse and realistic contact scenarios during training, resulting in controllers that exhibit natural adaptation on real deformable surfaces. Through hardware experiments, we demonstrate the system's capability for online terrain identification and adaptation across a wide range of surface stiffness.

</details>


### [298] [PVRA: A Pointwise Key-point Voting Framework for Robotic Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19968)
*Kulunu Samarawickrama,Roel Pieters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于3D关键点的模块化学习框架，用于学习渐进式装配中的装配依赖关系，从而从RGB-D输入推断可执行的动作输出，并在装配姿态估计数据集上训练和评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代计算机视觉已实现机器人装配操作的部分自主性，但渐进式装配的自主操作需要更具体的能力。通过比较分析，作者指出物体中心感知必须向学习装配依赖关系推进，以预测有意义的可操作输出。

Method: 提出一个3D关键点为基础的模块化学习框架，用于学习装配依赖关系，从RGB-D场景输入中推断可执行的动作输出。在装配姿态估计数据集上训练该网络，并与物体中心基线方法进行对比。

Result: 在装配姿态估计数据集上进行了训练和评估，使用针对渐进式装配的扩展指标集与物体中心基线方法进行了比较。

Conclusion: 物体中心感知需进阶为装配依赖学习，所提出的基于3D关键点的模块化框架能够有效地从感知中推断装配可执行动作。

Abstract: Modern computer vision has enabled partial autonomy in robotic assembly manipulation. However, performing autonomous manipulation of a progressive assembly demands a more specific set of skills, in addition to perceiving the objects. Through a comparative analysis of research in the associated domains, we deduce that object-centric perception must advance towards learning assembly dependencies to predict meaningful actionable outputs for autonomous assembly manipulation. Subsequently, we present a 3D keypoint-based modular learning framework to learn assembly dependencies to infer actionable outputs given a RGB-D input of an assembly scene. We train and evaluate our trained network on an assembly pose estimation dataset and compare it against object-centric baselines with an augmented set of metrics for progressive assemblies.

</details>


### [299] [Learning Highly Dynamic Skills Transition for Quadruped Jumping Through Constrained Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19977)
*Zeren Luo,Jiahui Zhang,Yimin Han,Ji Ma,Minghao Lu,Ioannis Havoutis,Peng Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种层级强化学习框架，使四足机器人能够模仿真实动物行为，通过视觉感知获取窄门信息并自主决策，完成穿越狭窄障碍物的爆发性运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有腿动物能够在狭窄空间中执行爆发性运动，但在四足机器人上复制这一行为一直是长期挑战。

Method: 采用层级强化学习框架：低层策略通过模仿学习训练，模仿真实动物行为并形成多样化技能集；高层控制器了解低层技能的能力边界，并通过视觉检测获取门体信息，决策出无碰撞轨迹的动态穿越动作。

Result: 该框架不仅能实现窄门穿越，还可扩展到其他高度动态的任务，是首批在地面行走机器人上实现自主敏捷空中门体穿越任务的工作之一。

Conclusion: 该研究将足式机器人的敏捷性提升至接近其生物对应物的水平，验证了层级强化学习在拓展机器人动态能力方面的有效性。

Abstract: Although legged animals are capable of performing explosive motions while traversing confined spaces, replicating this behavior in quadrupedal robots has been a longstanding challenge. Here, we propose a hierarchical reinforcement learning pipeline that empowers the robots to perform aggressive locomotion through constrained obstacles--a narrow gate. The imitation learning technique is used to train the low-level policy, which mimics the behaviors of real animals and forms a set of diverse skills. The high-level controller, having an awareness of the capability of low-level skills and acquiring the gate information via vision-based detection, determines the suitable maneuvers with collision-free trajectories to traverse it dynamically. Notably, we also verify that this framework can be extended to other highly dynamic tasks. This is one of the first works that perform autonomous and agile aerial gate traversal tasks on ground-walking robots, extending the lifelike agility of legged robots to match that of their biological counterparts.

</details>


### [300] [Evidence-Gated Task and Motion Planning with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20084)
*Tsunehiko Tanaka,Matthew Stephenson,Alistair Macvicar,Edgar Simo-Serra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EAFG框架，通过VLM生成的探索性子目标获取视觉证据，并结合TAMP执行，利用可行性门控判断是否继续任务规划、获取更多证据或停止，从而解决部分可观测条件下机器人长时程操作任务中目标物体不确定性导致的规划失败问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人从自然语言指令执行长时程操作任务时，需要同时推理语义任务结构和几何可行性。然而在部分可观测环境下，目标物体的可用性存在不确定性，VLM与TAMP结合的方法可能生成依赖先验知识但缺乏观测证据支持的子目标，导致执行失败或意外结果。

Method: EAFG框架包含两条核心机制：(1)证据获取：通过VLM生成的探索性子目标驱动TAMP执行，主动采集视觉证据以确认目标物体的可用性；(2)可行性门控：基于已获取的证据，决策系统选择继续任务规划、获取更多证据，或中止任务。

Result: 在物体使用存在歧义的烹饪任务中，EAFG通过规划前发现任务相关物体，提升了配方完成率；对于需要缺失物体的指令，EAFG能够促进恰当的中止决策，并减少反复尝试操作该物体的行为。

Conclusion: EAFG通过显式的证据获取与可行性门控机制，有效弥合了VLM先验知识与实际观测证据之间的差距，提升了部分可观测条件下机器人长时程操作任务的鲁棒性和可靠性。

Abstract: Robots executing long-horizon manipulation tasks from natural-language instructions must reason about both semantic task structure and geometric feasibility. However, under partial observability, the availability of goal-relevant objects may be uncertain. In such cases, approaches that combine Vision-Language Models (VLMs) with Task and Motion Planning (TAMP) may generate subgoals that rely on the VLM's prior knowledge without observational support, leading to execution failures or unintended outcomes. We propose Evidence Acquisition and Feasibility Gating (EAFG), a framework that acquires visual evidence through VLM-generated exploratory subgoals and TAMP-based execution. EAFG then applies a feasibility gate to decide whether to proceed with task planning, acquire further evidence, or halt. Our experiments show that, in cooking tasks with ambiguous object use, EAFG improves recipe completion by discovering task-relevant objects before planning. For instructions requiring an absent object, EAFG promotes appropriate halt decisions and reduces repeated attempts to manipulate that object.

</details>


### [301] [Towards Professional Tennis Styles for Humanoid Robots with Adaptive Motion Planning and Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20087)
*Tao Huang,Ruofei Liu,Xuchen Tang,Xinyin Zhang,Junli Ren,Huayi Wang,Feiyu Jia,Yukai Qi,Kangning Yin,Weishuai Zeng,Lipeng Chen,Xi Li,Ting Wu,Kailin Li,Ruoli Dai,Jingbo Wang,Lei Han,Jiangmiao Pang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaPT框架，一种自适应的运动规划与跟踪框架，直接从广播视频学习职业网球发球和回球风格，通过在规划器中引入运动速度适配器、在跟踪器中学习随机化执行速度的鲁棒跟踪，有效弥合了仿真到真实的差距，并在Unitree G1和全尺寸Dobot Atom人形机器人上完成了真实世界验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在真实世界球类运动中已展现出潜力，但如何在保持强大任务性能的同时实现专业级运动风格仍具挑战。此外，仿真中有效的分层设计在真实机器人上存在显著的sim-to-real差距：跟踪性能退化、自回归规划忽略了这种退化、以及噪声感知进一步加剧误差累积。

Method: 提出AdaPT分层框架：规划器生成风格化的运动学轨迹，跟踪器以最小干扰方式执行规划。针对sim-to-real差距，设计了两种自适应机制：(1) 跟踪器通过学习跟踪随机化的执行速度来提升鲁棒性；(2) 规划器条件化在一个学习的运动速度适配器上，以缓解复合误差问题。

Result: 在Unitree G1上的真实实验验证了自适应机制桥接sim-to-real差距的有效性；进一步将AdaPT策略部署到全尺寸Dobot Atom人形机器人（1.7m）上，在无动作捕捉的条件下实现了野外发球演示。

Conclusion: AdaPT证明了通过规划-跟踪分层设计配合速度自适应机制，可以使人形机器人从广播视频中学习专业运动风格并成功部署到真实场景。真实实验还揭示了未来人形球类运动系统在算法与工程方面的关键洞见。

Abstract: Humanoid robots have recently demonstrated promising capabilities in real-world ball sports. However, achieving professional motion styles while maintaining strong task performance remains challenging. In this work, we propose AdaPT, an Adaptive Motion Planning and Tracking framework that learns professional tennis serving and rally styles directly from broadcast videos. This hierarchical design is motivated by the key insight that the planner generates stylistic kinematic motions, while the tracker executes them with minimal interference with planning. Despite its effectiveness in simulation, a substantial sim-to-real gap emerges: tracking performance inevitably degrades on real robots, and this degradation is partially overlooked by autoregressive planning and further compounded by noisy perception. To address these issues, our adaptation mechanism improves tracking robustness by learning to track randomized execution speeds, while conditioning the planner on a learned motion-speed adapter to mitigate compounding errors. Real-world experiments on the Unitree G1 demonstrate the effectiveness of our adaptation mechanism in bridging the sim-to-real gap. We further deploy AdaPT policies on the full-size Dobot Atom humanoid robot (1.7m) and demonstrate in-the-wild serving without motion capture. Beyond these results, our real-world experiments reveal both algorithmic and engineering insights for future humanoid ball-sports systems. Videos and code are available on our \href{https://humanoidtennis.github.io/AdaPT/}{project website}.

</details>


### [302] [Planning-Oriented End-to-End Autonomous Driving: Architectures, Evaluation, and Emerging Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20111)
*Yanchen Guan,Xingcheng Liu,Bin Rao,Chengyue Wang,Guofa Li,Yunjian Li,Lishengsa Yue,Zhiyong Cui,Chengzhong Xu,Zhenning Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了端到端自动驾驶从"摄像头到控制"的回归范式向"以规划为导向"的系统转型，从行为克隆、条件模仿学习、特权蒸馏、BEV/矢量规划、统一感知-预测-规划架构、世界模型规划器到视觉-语言-动作系统，提出现代端到端驾驶的核心区别不在于是否使用中间表示，而在于这些表示是否被学习、监督和评估以支持安全、可行和路线合规的规划，并沿输入表示、规划输出、监督信号和评估协议四个轴组织文献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶领域发展迅速，但架构形式多样且评估标准不统一，导致难以在不同方法之间进行有效比较和判断进展。作者旨在厘清该领域的关键区分维度，并揭示仅依赖基于位移的开放环路度量无法为安全、人类对齐的驾驶提供充分证据这一核心问题。

Method: 采用综述方法，沿四个分析轴（输入表示、规划输出、监督信号、评估协议）系统整理现有方法，涵盖行为克隆、条件模仿学习、特权蒸馏、BEV与矢量规划、统一感知-预测-规划架构、世界模型规划器及视觉-语言-动作系统等类别，并考察了从开放环路轨迹匹配到闭环仿真、非反应式真实日志评估、长尾测试及人类偏好感知度量等基准演变趋势。

Result: 分析表明架构进步在缺乏基准一致的评估下难以解释；基于位移的开放环路指标对安全且人类对齐的驾驶仅提供有限证据；揭示出领域中的关键基准转变趋势，并识别了架构创新与评估方法之间的不匹配问题。

Conclusion: 文章总结出当前领域仍面临六大开放挑战：不确定性感知规划、学习者与专家不匹配、运行时安全保证、语言与动作接地、世界模型验证以及可复现基准测试，为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving has evolved from camera-to-control regression toward planning-oriented systems that use structured representations, trajectory-level outputs, and increasingly realistic evaluation protocols. This survey reviews this transition across behavior cloning, conditional imitation learning, privileged distillation, BEV and vectorized planning, unified perception-prediction-planning architectures, world-model-based planners, and vision-language-action systems. We argue that the key distinction in modern end-to-end driving is not whether intermediate representations are used, but whether they are learned, supervised, and evaluated to support safe, feasible, and route-compliant planning.
