To organize the literature, we synthesize existing methods along four axes: input representation, planning output, supervision signal, and evaluation protocol. We further examine the benchmark shift from open-loop trajectory matching to closed-loop simulation, non-reactive real-log evaluation, long-tail testing, and human-preference-aware metrics. Our analysis highlights that architectural progress is difficult to interpret without benchmark-consistent evaluation, and that displacement-based open-loop metrics alone provide limited evidence for safe and human-aligned driving. We conclude with open challenges in uncertainty-aware planning, learner-expert mismatch, runtime safety assurance, language-action grounding, world-model validation, and reproducible benchmarking.

</details>


### [303] [Video2DoorTraversal: Push Door Traversal via Simulated Door Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20251)
*Xincheng Tang,Yiji Chen,Youhan Xie,Wanyu Li,Zhengjie Shu,Lai Jiang,Wenkang Hu,Yitong Li,Jinchuang Zhang,Xibin Song,Ruigang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了Video2DoorTraversal，一个基于单视频真实到仿真到真实（real-to-sim-to-real）框架的轮腿移动机械臂开门穿越系统。利用DoorTwin从单段RGB视频重建实例对齐的关节仿真门，通过仿真在环智能体生成可执行演示，训练ArticuACT双深度策略，实现多门高成功率与零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开门并穿越是一项长视距locomotion-manipulation（运动-操作）任务，需要精确的把手交互与底座-手臂协调控制。传统方法难以从单条视频中同时恢复门的关关节结构并生成可执行策略，缺乏利用真实视频数据驱动仿真训练与策略学习的通用框架。

Method: 1) DoorTwin：从单段RGB视频重建实例对齐、可关关节运动、仿真就绪的门孪生模型，保留真实几何与外观；2) 仿真在环智能体将恢复的关节运动转化为参数化技能程序，通过迭代优化失败的rollout生成物理上可执行的演示；3) ArticuACT：双深度策略，以机器人中心相机条件与交互感知监督预测底座、手臂与夹爪的协调指令；4) 全部感知与策略推理均板载运行。

Result: 在五扇真实门上平均成功率达96.57%，对结构相似的未见门零样本成功率达80.95%，完成接近、开门与穿越的完整流程平均约13秒。

Conclusion: Video2DoorTraversal展示了通过在环仿真与实例对齐的重建，单段视频即可驱动轮腿移动机械臂实现高可靠性的开门穿越任务，并具备较好的零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Door opening and traversal is a long-horizon loco-manipulation task that requires precise handle interaction and coordinated base-arm control. We present Video2DoorTraversal, a single-video real-to-sim-to-real framework for wheel-legged mobile manipulators. Given one RGB video of a real door, DoorTwin reconstructs an instance-aligned, articulated, and simulation-ready door twin with realistic geometry and appearance. A simulation-in-the-loop agent converts the recovered articulation into a parameterized skill program and iteratively refines failed rollouts to generate physically executable demonstrations. These demonstrations are used to train ArticuACT, a dual-depth policy that predicts coordinated base, arm, and gripper commands using robot-centric camera conditioning and interaction-aware supervision. With all perception and policy inference running onboard, the system achieves a 96.57% average success rate across five real doors and an 80.95% zero-shot success rate on structurally similar unseen doors, while completing the full approach, opening, and traversal sequence in approximately 13s on average. Project Page: https://video2doortraversal.github.io/.

</details>


### [304] [DART-S: Reachability-Audited Active-Suspension Preconditioning for Off-Road Vehicle Jumps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20275)
*Yu Hu,Fangzhou Zhao,Liang Chen,Chen Min,Wei Li,Mingyuan Sang,Jiajia Ma,Shican Chen,Di Pang,Baolei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DART-S通过坡面悬挂预调节(preconditioning)在起飞前改变俯仰、俯仰角速度和车轮自旋，从而转移查询状态并重分配角动量预算，使飞行中的反扭矩无法纠正的起飞误差在起飞前就被消除。在72个独立BeamNG会话的600次新运行中，所有查询均按预设分支执行；在确认的边界条件下，DART-S达到24/24的姿态判据成功率，而基线DART为0/24。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飞行中的反扭矩纠正能力受限于车轮角动量预算，起飞时产生的误差（如俯仰姿态偏差）一旦超出预算就无法在飞行阶段修复。因此需要在起飞前对状态进行预调节，以改变剩余权限预算，从根本上解决起飞误差不可恢复的问题。

Method: DART-S采用三方面机制：(1) 坡面悬挂预调节：在起飞前改变俯仰、俯仰角速度和车轮自旋，重塑查询状态与权限预算对；(2) 局部校准映射：预测每个悬挂动作如何重塑状态-预算对；(3) 支持感知选择器：将预测偏移与局部结果证据及区间可达性筛查相结合，并由精确配对审计报告剩余权限，确保正、负、边界查询均走预设分支。

Result: 在72个独立BeamNG会话共600次新运行中，所有正、负和边界查询均严格遵循预设分支。在40°/13 m/s边界条件下，DART-S达到24/24的着陆后姿态判据成功率（基线DART为0/24，会话级Holm校正p=0.0234）；在11.5 m/s速度下，0.35秒定时动作达到23/24成功率（静态预设为0/24，p=0.0156）；200 rad/s命令防护使600次运行中传动系统硬限制超限次数为零。

Conclusion: DART-S通过起飞前的悬挂预调节策略有效解决了飞行阶段反扭矩纠正能力不足的问题，在边界条件下实现了从0%到100%的姿态成功率的显著提升，且具备完整的运行安全性保障，代码将开源发布。

Abstract: Airborne torque reaction cannot recover takeoff errors beyond the wheel angular-momentum budget. DART-S applies ramp-face suspension preconditioning to change pitch, pitch rate, and wheel spin before liftoff, thereby shifting the queried state and altering the remaining authority budget. To predict how each suspension action reshapes this state-budget pair, DART-S employs a local calibration map. A support-aware selector combines the predicted shift with local outcome evidence and an interval-reachability screen; an exact-pair audit reports residual authority. Across 600 new runs in 72 independent BeamNG sessions, every positive, negative, and boundary query follows its prespecified branch. At the confirmed 40°/13 m/s boundary, DART-S attains 24/24 post-touchdown attitude-criterion successes versus 0/24 for DART (session-level Holm-adjusted p=0.0234). At 11.5 m/s, a 0.35 s timing action attains 23/24 versus 0/24 for the static preset (p=0.0156). The 200 rad/s command guard keeps drivetrain hard-limit exceedance at zero across all 600 runs. The source code will be available at https://github.com/MeridianCAS/DART-S

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [305] [The Lazy Pod That Lies: Deferred Cost and Failure Semantics of Lazy Container Image Pulling for Model Serving on Kubernetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19412)
*Georgii Kliukovkin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估了Kubernetes上两种生产级懒加载容器镜像拉取系统（eStargz/stargz-snapshotter和AWS SOCI）在模型推理服务场景下的表现，发现懒加载虽然实现了冷启动时延与模型大小无关（16.9-17.6秒vs 24.5-573.0秒的急切拉取），但成本被推迟而非消除，并揭示了急切拉取不会出现的失败模式：节点级缓存耗尽导致已运行Pod读取模型文件失败，且存在Kubernetes可见性与实际故障之间存在196秒的盲区，以及守护进程重启导致永久陈旧文件句柄的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容器镜像懒加载被视为消除模型服务Pod启动主导成本的关键技术，但其在生产模型交付场景中的真实表现、代价转移方式及潜在的失败模式尚缺乏系统的实证评估，亟需深入理解以为推理平台设计提供指导。

Method: 在Kubernetes上使用KServe，对eStargz/stargz-snapshotter和AWS SOCI两种生产级懒加载系统，与急切拉取基线进行对比实验；使用包含真实fp16权重的2至140GB工件，测量冷启动时延、完整读取性能，并系统表征节点级缓存耗尽下的失败模式及其Kubernetes可观测性盲区。

Result: 懒加载实现了冷时延与大小无关（16.9-17.6s vs 24.5-573.0s）；但14GB模型的懒加载完整读取耗时105.3s（慢于急切拉取的72.4s）。两种系统在生命周期不同端支付成本（SOCI在Ready前预取近全部镜像，eStargz将几乎一切推迟到首次读取）。关键发现：在持续合法读取下，节点级缓存耗尽导致运行中Pod读取失败；最早阶段存在196秒的Kubernetes可见性盲区，重压下67-94%模型文件失败；缓存释放可自愈，但守护进程重启导致运行中Pod的永久陈旧文件句柄。

Conclusion: 懒加载兑现了冷启动加速的核心承诺，但运维方必须认识到其成本是推迟而非消除，并针对缓存耗尽这一急切拉取结构上不可能出现的失败模式，制定缓存放置、监控和缓存容量规划策略，以保障推理服务的可靠性。

Abstract: Lazy container-image pulling promises to eliminate the dominant cost of starting a model-serving pod by mounting the image immediately and fetching content on demand. We evaluate this promise for model delivery on Kubernetes, using KServe with two production lazy-pulling systems -- eStargz/stargz-snapshotter and AWS SOCI -- against eager baselines, on artifacts from 2 to 140 GB including real fp16 weights. Lazy pulling delivers its headline: cold time-to-first-prediction becomes size-independent (16.9--17.6s, versus 24.5--573.0s eager). But the cost is deferred, not eliminated: a full read of a 14 GB model through the lazy mount takes 105.3s, slower than the 72.4s eager pull it replaced, and the two systems pay at opposite lifecycle ends (SOCI prefetches nearly the full image before Ready; eStargz defers nearly everything to first read). More consequentially, we characterize a failure mode eager pulling structurally cannot exhibit: under sustained legitimate reads with default configuration, the snapshotter's node-level cache exhausts its finite volume and already-running pods begin failing reads of model files. At the earliest stage of exhaustion, an instrumented serving pod passed every Kubernetes-visible and application-level check for 196s while its snapshotter was already logging real failures; under heavier pressure, 67--94% of model files fail, scaling monotonically with residual cache occupancy. A live pod self-heals if cache space is freed under it, but a snapshotter-daemon restart under a live pod leaves permanently stale file handles in a pod still reported Running. We derive placement, monitoring, and cache-sizing guidance for serving platforms and operators.

</details>


### [306] [When Do LLM Agents Help? Deadline-Aware Mixed-Criticality Task Scheduling at the Autonomous-Vehicle Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19557)
*Reza Zakerian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究异构MEC服务器上截止时限感知的混合关键性调度问题，构建了一个窗口化合同网拍卖启发式算法（TC完成率0.902，优于所有基线），并通过受控消融分析证明LLM控制面仅在非平稳负载（安全关键突发）下才产生显著收益，静态负载下启发式近乎最优，LLM编排的额外成本只在固定策略无法利用的动态窗口期才值得付出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆将延迟敏感感知任务卸载至MEC服务器，关键任务失败意味着不安全而非降级。LLM被越来越多地提议作为自适应、可解释的调度器，但缺乏其何时真正有效的证据，因此需要严格评估LLM控制层相对于强启发式的增量价值。

Method: 分两步构建：首先设计强基线启发式——窗口化合同网拍卖，按最早截止时间关键优先排序每个准入窗口，按最早完成时间放置任务；其次叠加多智能体LLM控制平面。通过受控消融分解将调度器优势归因于两个普通因素（批处理窗口和关键优先排序），并在60个实例、3种拓扑、15个基线下进行对比验证，测试平稳负载与中途安全关键任务突增两种场景。

Result: 启发式算法在相同在线约束下达到0.902的TC任务完成率，优于所有15个基线（Holm校正p<0.001，最佳基线为0.838），达到CP-SAT上界的0.87。消融显示批处理窗口和关键优先排序是两个主导因素，拍卖机制、窗口级LLM策略和在线自适应在平稳负载下无增益；但在安全关键任务中程突增的非平稳场景中，LLM控制平面显著优于静态启发式和bandit方法。

Conclusion: LLM编排的价值是有条件的：仅当非平稳性为固定策略无法利用的调度腾出操作空间时，其成本才被证明是合理的。论文公开了控制平面延迟、推理依据以及全部代码和带种子的实例。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles offload latency-sensitive perception tasks to nearby mobile edge computing (MEC) servers, where a missed safety-critical task is unsafe rather than merely degraded. Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly proposed as adaptive, explainable schedulers, yet evidence of when they help is scarce. We study deadline-aware, mixed-criticality scheduling on heterogeneous MEC servers, where time-critical (TC) tasks must be protected at a controlled cost to best-effort traffic, and ask whether a multi-agent LLM control layer improves on a strong heuristic. We answer in two steps. First we build the heuristic: a windowed contract-net auction that orders each admission window time-critical-first by earliest deadline and places tasks by earliest-finish-time. Across 60 instances on three topologies and 15 baselines under an identical online constraint, it attains a TC completion rate of 0.902, above every baseline (Holm-corrected p < 0.001; best baseline 0.838) and at 0.87 of a CP-SAT upper bound. Second, we add the LLM control plane. A controlled decomposition traces the scheduler's advantage to two ordinary factors, the batching horizon and time-critical-first ordering; the auction, the per-window LLM policy, and online adaptation add nothing while the load is stationary, where the heuristic is already near-optimal. Under a mid-run surge of safety-critical tasks the picture changes, and the LLM control plane gains significantly over both the static heuristic and the bandit. LLM orchestration therefore earns its cost only when non-stationarity opens headroom a fixed policy cannot use. We report control-plane latency and rationale, and release all code and seeded instances.

</details>


### [307] [Optimality and Trade-offs in Fast BFT SMR (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19629)
*Neil Giridharan,Minh Tung Nguyen,Ittai Abraham,Allen Clement,Natacha Crooks,Pierre Sutra*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了崩溃容错设定下快速状态机复制（Fast SMR）在拜占庭容错（BFT）设定中的复制因子上下界，提出了严格的复制因子上限和下限，并展示了一个在复制因子与恢复效率之间存在权衡的次优协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速SMR协议在崩溃容错领域已得到广泛研究，具有低延迟、高吞吐、高可用性和公平性等优势，但在拜占庭容错设定下的研究仍然不足，仅存在少数的Fast BFT SMR协议，需要对其复制因子要求进行系统性的理论分析。

Method: 通过理论分析推导Fast BFT SMR所需复制因子的严格（tight）上界和下界，并设计一个次优协议以展示复制因子与恢复效率之间的权衡关系。

Result: 给出了Fast BFT SMR复制因子的严格上界和下界，并提出了一种次优协议来实证展示复制因子与恢复效率之间的trade-off。

Conclusion: Fast BFT SMR的复制因子存在严格的理论界限，且在实际协议设计中需要在复制因子与恢复效率之间进行权衡取舍。

Abstract: Fast state-machine replication (SMR) protocols in the crash-fault setting have attracted significant interest in both academia and industry. This interest stems from their advantages over leader-based protocols, including low execution latency for non-conflicting commands, high throughput, improved availability, and increased fairness. While Fast SMR is well studied in the crash-fault setting, the Byzantine fault-tolerant (BFT) setting remains much less understood, with only a small number of existing Fast BFT SMR protocols. In this paper, we present tight upper and lower bounds on the replication factor required for Fast BFT SMR. We also present a suboptimal protocol that illustrates a trade-off between replication factor and recovery efficiency.

</details>


### [308] [CacheRoute: Planned Prefix-Affinity Routing for Large-Scale LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19677)
*Huang Cheng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CacheRoute通过周期性的路由规划来平衡前缀缓存亲和性与负载均衡之间的冲突，在保持热门键缓存命中率的同时避免服务器过载，在Llama-3.3-70B fp8的60块H100集群上实现了2.3倍于最强基线的吞吐量，并将KV缓存命中率从64.1%提升至93.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前缀缓存只有在重复请求返回仍持有前缀KV的服务器时才能避免预填充。缓存盲均衡会分散这种复用机会，而固定亲和性虽能保留复用但可能导致服务器过载。现有方法无法同时兼顾缓存亲和性与负载均衡。

Method: CacheRoute采用周期性的路由规划方案：将高请求率的热键限入一个稳定的热集（warm set），根据期望负载分配其目标服务器。热键可以使用多个目标，以灵活平衡负载，同时通过维护热键的KV缓存亲和性来最大化缓存命中率。研究还通过多组对照实验分离亲和性与放置策略的效果，并采用影子回放（shadow replay）来门控部署。

Result: 在Llama-3.3-70B fp8、60块H100 GPU上，CacheRoute在3.5秒p99 SLO下维持176±11 QPS，是五种基线中最好的2.3倍；KV缓存命中率从缓存盲均衡下的64.1±1.3%提升至93.2±0.5%。半合成聚合数据及8B与突发性对照实验验证了方法与亲和性和放置策略效果的分离。然而，两个32B工作负载表明，当亲和性恢复的KV工作量太少时，残余的负载偏斜会削弱甚至消除改进。

Conclusion: CacheRoute有效解决了缓存亲和性与负载均衡的权衡，但其效果依赖于亲和性能够恢复足够的KV工作量。当亲和性收益有限时，负载偏斜会抵消其优势。因此，建议在任何部署前使用影子回放进行门控验证，而非仅凭工作负载统计特征启用亲和性。

Abstract: Prefix caching avoids prefill only when a repeated request returns to a server that still holds the prefix KV. Cache-blind balancing disperses that reuse; fixed affinity preserves it but can overload a server. CacheRoute resolves this tradeoff with a periodic routing plan. It admits high-rate keys to a stable warm set and places their assignments by expected load. Hot keys may use more than one destination, although every key in our primary semi-synthetic aggregate uses exactly one. On Llama-3.3-70B in fp8 across 60 H100 GPUs, CacheRoute sustains 176+/-11 QPS at a 3.5-s p99 SLO, 2.3x the strongest of five baselines. Served KV-cache hit rate rises from 64.1+/-1.3% under cache-blind balancing to 93.2+/-0.5%. A second semi-synthetic aggregate and controlled 8B and burst experiments separate the effects of affinity and placement. Two 32B workloads provide the counterexamples: when affinity recovers too little KV work, its residual load skew reduces or erases the improvement. We therefore recommend gating any deployment with a shadow replay rather than enabling affinity from workload statistics alone.

</details>


### [309] [ParaWeb: Parallel Programming Patterns for Web Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19935)
*Suejb Memeti*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ParaWeb，一个TypeScript库，实现了十种并行编程模式，涵盖Node.js Worker Threads、浏览器Web Workers和WebGPU计算着色器三种执行环境。每种模式提供消息传递（MP）、共享缓冲区（Shared）和GPU三种变体，共30种实现。实验表明CPU变体最高提速11.6倍，GPU变体最高提速260倍，图像卷积案例中GPU加速可达414倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JavaScript虽然是Web主导语言，但传统上受限于单线程执行模型。Node.js Worker Threads和浏览器Web Workers虽然提供了底层并行机制，但缺乏捕获常见并行结构的高级抽象，开发者无法将这些结构作为可复用模式使用。

Method: ParaWeb库为每种并行模式提供三种实现变体：(1)基于postMessage结构化克隆的消息传递（MP）变体；(2)使用SharedArrayBuffer和类型化数组视图的共享缓冲区（Shared）变体；(3)基于WebGPU计算着色器的GPU变体。实验评估了所有30种实现在三种数据规模下的性能，涵盖服务器端Node.js、浏览器端环境和WebGPU计算着色器。

Result: CPU变体在16线程下对计算密集型模式达到最高11.6倍加速；GPU变体对高算术强度的计算密集型模式（如Farm、Scatter、Reduce和Map）达到最高260倍加速。在五个图像卷积滤波器的案例研究中，GPU加速对不可分离核达到最高414倍加速，并在1024x1024、2048x2048和4K图像上呈现一致的可扩展性。

Conclusion: ParaWeb展示了在高抽象层次上提供可复用并行模式库的可行性，使JavaScript/TypeScript开发者能够通过高层模式而非底层原语来利用多线程和GPU硬件加速，显著提升Web应用中计算密集型任务的性能。

Abstract: Modern web applications increasingly require computationally intensive processing, yet JavaScript, the dominant language of the web, has traditionally been limited to a single-threaded execution model. Node.js Worker Threads and browser Web Workers provide low-level mechanisms for parallel execution, but developers lack high-level abstractions that capture recurring parallel structures as reusable patterns. In this paper, we present ParaWeb, a TypeScript library that implements ten parallel programming patterns for server-side Node.js, client-side browser environments, and WebGPU compute shaders. ParaWeb provides three implementation variants for each pattern: a message-passing (MP) variant based on structured cloning via postMessage, a shared-buffer (Shared) variant that uses SharedArrayBuffer with typed array views, and a GPU variant that uses WebGPU compute shaders for hardware-accelerated execution. We describe the architecture, design decisions, and pattern-specific implementation strategies, and we evaluate the performance of all thirty implementations across three data sizes. Experimental evaluation results show that the CPU-based variants achieve speedups of up to 11.6x with 16 threads for compute-bound patterns, while the GPU variants reach speedups of up to 260x for compute-bound patterns with high arithmetic intensity such as Farm, Scatter, Reduce, and Map. A case study on five image-convolution filters further shows that GPU acceleration reaches up to 414x speedup over single-threaded CPU on non-separable kernels, with consistent scaling across 1024x1024$, 2048x2048$, and 4K images.

</details>


### [310] [Design and Empirical Evaluation of a Network-Centric, On-Premises Architecture for Earth Observation Data Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20283)
*João Pinelo,João Gonçalves,Denis Willett,Amit Ruhela,Derek Steinmoeller,Uriel Mendoza,Pelumi S. Alao,Ronald Soares Lopes,Rogerio Atem de Carvalho,Pedro Mattos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一种面向本地（on-premises）地球观测（EO）数据访问的、以网络为中心的可复制架构，该架构已在AIR数据中心（大西洋云计划的首个节点）完成首次实际部署。系统由100 GbE网络上的MinIO对象存储集群、PostGIS元数据目录和OGC API-EDR访问层组成。研究通过严格隔离网络带宽为唯一变量的对照实验，揭示了网络带宽是批量EO数据访问中存储吞吐量的主导约束（直至阈值），超过阈值后端点内存拓扑成为决定因素。该阈值在本硬件类别中约为每服务器10 Gbps。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测项目产生的数据量远超多数机构网络的传输与存储能力。公共云平台仅惠及资源充足的组织，而大西洋盆地两岸的机构受限于网络连通性、数据主权和资金约束，只能依赖本地基础设施。云原生数据格式虽支持高效的部分读取，但其性能取决于底层网络带宽，而这一依赖关系很少被独立测量。因此需要一项针对本地EO数据访问的系统性、可量化的网络性能研究。

Method: 作者构建了一个以网络为中心的本地EO数据访问系统，包括：(1) MinIO对象存储集群部署于100 GbE网络设备；(2) PostGIS元数据目录；(3) OGC API-EDR访问层。实验方法上：在持续并行负载下表征网络设备性能；评估对象存储面向EO代表性工作负载的吞吐量；在相同硬件上比较实测性能与限速（throttled）基线，将网络带宽隔离为唯一变量；并通过多站点复制基准测试与伙伴机构合作，刻画模型所依赖的联合（federation）原语。

Result: 研究发现：网络带宽是批量EO数据访问中存储吞吐量的主导约束，直至某一阈值；超过该阈值后，决定系统可用带宽的是端点内存拓扑而非容量。在本硬件类别中，该阈值位于每服务器10 Gbps以上。低于该阈值时，网络容量单独决定设施可交付的性能；高于该阈值时，进一步网络投资回报取决于端点内存配置，而内存配置的升级可以推迟到日后进行。

Conclusion: 该架构为网络受限且资源有限的机构提供了一条可行的本地EO数据服务路径。研究明确了网络带宽与内存拓扑在不同性能区间的主导关系，为硬件投资决策（如网络升级与内存升级的时序安排）提供了量化依据。首次部署于AIR数据中心验证了架构的可复制性和实用价值。

Abstract: Earth observation (EO) programmes generate data at volumes that exceed the transfer and storage capacity of most institutional networks. Public cloud platforms address this for well-resourced organisations, but institutions across the Atlantic basin face constraints in connectivity, sovereignty and funding that make on-premises infrastructure the only viable path. Cloud-native data formats enable efficient partial reads, yet their performance depends on the bandwidth of the underlying network fabric, a dependency rarely measured in isolation. This paper presents a replicable, network-centric architecture for on-premises EO data access, evaluated at its first operational deployment: the AIR Data Centre, founding node of the Atlantic Cloud. The system comprises a MinIO object storage cluster on a 100 GbE fabric, a PostGIS metadata catalogue and an OGC API-EDR access layer. We characterise the fabric under sustained parallel load, evaluate object storage throughput for EO-representative workloads, and compare measured performance against throttled baselines on identical hardware, isolating network bandwidth as the sole variable. Multi-site replication benchmarks with partner institutions characterise the federation primitive the model depends on. Network bandwidth is the dominant constraint on storage throughput for bulk EO data access up to a threshold; beyond it, endpoint memory topology rather than capacity governs how much bandwidth a system can use. For this hardware class that threshold lies above 10 Gbps per server. Below it, network capacity alone sets what the facility can deliver; above it, the return on further network investment depends on endpoint memory provisioning, which can be deferred and bought later.

</details>
